                  orientation: "portrait",
                },
                buffer,
              },
            ],
            totalAmount: 5,
            paymentMethod: "cash",
            paymentStatus: isLegacyConfirmed ? "confirmed" : "pending",
            enqueuedAt: new Date(),
          },
          assignmentsRef.current
        );
      },

      onSessionError: (sessionId, error) => {
        console.error(`[App] Session ${sessionId} error:`, error);
        setActiveJob((prev) =>
          prev && prev.sessionId === sessionId
            ? { ...prev, status: "error", detail: error }
            : null
        );
      },
    });

    signalingChannelRef.current = channel;

    return () => {
      console.log(`[App] Tearing down signaling channel...`);
      signalingChannelRef.current = null;
      stopShopSignaling(channel);
      setIsListening(false);
    };
  }, [isAuthenticated, shopId, isShopOnline, loadTodayPrintsCount, refreshAssignments]);

  // Payment Confirmation Handler called from LiveDashboard
  const handleConfirmPayment = useCallback(
    async (sessionId: string) => {
      console.log(`[App] Confirming payment and enqueuing for session ${sessionId}...`);
      addBoundedSet(confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current, sessionId);
      fifoDispatcherRef.current?.updateJobPaymentStatus(sessionId, "confirmed");

      // Find the job in the pendingQueue and actually enqueue it to print
      setPendingQueue(prev => {
        const jobIndex = prev.findIndex(j => j.sessionId === sessionId);
        if (jobIndex >= 0) {
          const job = prev[jobIndex];
          fifoDispatcherRef.current?.enqueue(job, assignmentsRef.current);
          return prev.filter((_, i) => i !== jobIndex);
        }
        return prev;
      });

      setActiveJob((prev) => {
        if (!prev || prev.sessionId !== sessionId) return prev;
        return {
          ...prev,
          paymentStatus: "confirmed",
          paymentConfirmedAt: new Date(),
        };
      });

      setRecentJobs((prev) =>
        prev.map((job) =>
          job.sessionId === sessionId
            ? {
                ...job,
                paymentStatus: "confirmed",
                paymentConfirmedAt: new Date(),
              }
            : job
        )
      );

      const targetJob =
        (activeJobRef.current?.sessionId === sessionId ? activeJobRef.current : null) ||
        recentJobsRef.current.find((j) => j.sessionId === sessionId);

      // 1. Broadcast payment-confirmed event to customer over signaling channel
      if (signalingChannelRef.current) {
        try {
          await broadcastPaymentConfirmed(
            signalingChannelRef.current,
            sessionId,
            targetJob?.tokenCode
          );
          console.log(`[App] Broadcasted payment-confirmed to customer!`);
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn("[App] Error broadcasting payment confirmation:", err);
        }
      }

      // 2. Update Supabase print_orders table: marked confirmed and completed
      try {
        const supabase = getSupabase();
        await supabase
          .from("print_orders")
          .update({
            payment_status: "confirmed",
            status: "picked_up",
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
          .eq("session_id", sessionId);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[App] Error updating print_orders in Supabase:", err);
      }
    },
    []
  );

  // Cancel Order Handler
  const handleCancelOrder = useCallback(
    async (sessionId: string) => {
      console.log(`[App] Cancelling order for session ${sessionId}...`);
      setPendingQueue(prev => prev.filter(j => j.sessionId !== sessionId));
      
      setActiveJob((prev) => prev?.sessionId === sessionId ? null : prev);
      setRecentJobs(prev => prev.map(job => job.sessionId === sessionId ? { ...job, status: "cancelled" } : job));

      if (signalingChannelRef.current) {
         try {
            await broadcastOrderStatus(signalingChannelRef.current, {
               sessionId,
               status: "cancelled"
            });
         } catch (err) {
            console.warn("[App] Error broadcasting cancel status to customer:", err);
         }
      }
      
      try {
        const supabase = getSupabase();
        await supabase
          .from("print_orders")
          .update({
            status: "cancelled",
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
          .eq("session_id", sessionId);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[App] Error cancelling print_orders in Supabase:", err);
      }
    },
    []
  );

  // Retry Failed Print Job Handler called from LiveDashboard
  const handleRetryJob = useCallback(
    (sessionId: string) => {
      if (!fifoDispatcherRef.current || !shopId) return;
      refreshAssignments(shopId);
      const success = fifoDispatcherRef.current.retryJob(sessionId, assignmentsRef.current);
      if (success) {
        console.log(`[App] Retrying failed print job: ${sessionId}`);
        setActiveJob((prev) => {
          if (!prev || prev.sessionId !== sessionId) return prev;
          return {
            ...prev,
            status: "printing",
            detail: "Retrying print job on hardware spooler...",
          };
        });
      }
    },
    [shopId, refreshAssignments]
  );

  // Dismiss Failed Job Handler
  const handleDismissJob = useCallback(
    (sessionId: string) => {
      fifoDispatcherRef.current?.dismissFailedJob(sessionId);
      setActiveJob((prev) => (prev?.sessionId === sessionId ? null : prev));
    },
    []
  );

  // Loading state
  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div
        className="login-container"
        style={{
          flexDirection: "column",
          gap: 16,
          color: "var(--text-secondary)",
          fontSize: 14,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: 32,
            height: 32,
            border: "2px solid #16181D",
            borderTopColor: "transparent",
            borderRadius: "50%",
            animation: "spin 0.8s linear infinite",
          }}
        />
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", fontSize: 13, letterSpacing: "0.04em" }}>
          Initializing Terminal...
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Unauthenticated -> Login page
  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Login />;
  }

  // Authenticated -> Full Shop Admin Shell
  return (
    <div className={`app-shell ${!isSidebarOpen ? "sidebar-collapsed" : ""}`}>
      {isSidebarOpen && (
        <Sidebar
          currentPage={currentPage}
          onSelectPage={handleSelectPage}
          isOnline={isShopOnline}
          onToggleOnline={handleToggleOnline}
          isTogglingOnline={isTogglingOnline}
          onLogoutRequest={handleLogoutRequest}
          onHideSidebar={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)}
          tokenBalance={tokenBalance}
          tokenAlert={tokenAlert}
        />
      )}

      <div className="main-workspace">
        <div className="window-drag-strip">
          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="no-drag sidebar-toggle-btn"
              onClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen((prev) => !prev)}
              title={isSidebarOpen ? "Hide sidebar (Alt+S)" : "Show sidebar (Alt+S)"}
              aria-label={isSidebarOpen ? "Hide sidebar" : "Show sidebar"}
            >
              <IconSidebarToggle size={15} />
              <span className="sidebar-toggle-text">{isSidebarOpen ? "Hide Menu" : "Show Menu"}</span>
            </button>

            <div className="window-drag-title">
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: "#09090b" }}>Printap Agent</span>
              <span style={{ color: "var(--text-faint)" }}>•</span>
              <span style={{ color: "var(--text-secondary)", textTransform: "capitalize" }}>
                {currentPage === "live"
                  ? "Live Monitor"
                  : currentPage === "history"
                  ? "Print History"
                  : currentPage === "printers"
                  ? "Printers Control"
                  : currentPage === "services"
                  ? "Services & Rates"
                  : currentPage === "analytics"
                  ? "Revenue & Analytics"
                  : currentPage === "qr"
                  ? "Counter QR Stand"
                  : currentPage === "tokens"
                  ? "Token Management"
                  : "Shop Profile"}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div
            className="no-drag"
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 8,
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: 500,
              color:
                isShopOnline && isListening
                  ? "var(--accent-emerald)"
                  : "var(--text-muted)",
            }}
          >
            <span
              className={`beacon-dot ${
                isShopOnline && isListening ? "online" : "offline"
              }`}
              role="status"
              aria-label={
                isShopOnline && isListening
                  ? "Shop Online • P2P Active"
                  : "Shop Offline (Standby)"
              }
              style={{
                background:
                  isShopOnline && isListening
                    ? "var(--accent-emerald)"
                    : "var(--text-muted)",
                boxShadow:
                  isShopOnline && isListening
                    ? "0 0 8px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.4)"
                    : "none",
              }}
            />
            <span>
              {isShopOnline
                ? isListening
                  ? "Shop Online • P2P Active"
                  : "Connecting P2P..."
                : "Shop Offline (Standby)"}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <main style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", overflow: "hidden", position: "relative" }}>
          <div
            style={{
              display: currentPage === "live" ? "flex" : "none",
              flex: 1,
              flexDirection: "column",
              height: "100%",
              width: "100%",
              overflow: "hidden",
            }}
          >
            <LiveDashboard
              currentJob={activeJob}
              recentJobs={recentJobs}
              pendingQueue={pendingQueue}
              todayPrintsCount={todayPrintsCount}
              isListening={isListening}
              connectedPrintersCount={
                printers.filter((p) => p.isPhysical && p.isOnline).length
              }
              onConfirmPayment={handleConfirmPayment}
              onCancelOrder={handleCancelOrder}
              onRetryJob={handleRetryJob}
              onDismissJob={handleDismissJob}
              onNavigateToHistory={() => handleSelectPage("history")}
              tokenBalance={tokenBalance}
              tokenAlert={tokenAlert}
            />
          </div>

          {currentPage === "history" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <PrintHistory recentJobs={recentJobs} />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "printers" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <PrintersControl
                printers={printers}
                isLoadingPrinters={isLoadingPrinters}
                onRescanPrinters={rescanPrinters}
                onAssignmentsSaved={(newAssignments) => {
                  assignmentsRef.current = newAssignments;
                }}
                onRegisterDirtyHandler={(handler) => {
                  dirtyHandlersRef.current["printers"] = handler;
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "services" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <ServicesRates
                onRegisterDirtyHandler={(handler) => {
                  dirtyHandlersRef.current["services"] = handler;
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "analytics" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <Analytics />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "qr" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <CounterQr />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "tokens" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <Tokens />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "profile" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <Profile onNavigateToQr={() => handleSelectPage("qr")} />
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>

      {toastMessage && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: "fixed",
            bottom: 24,
            right: 24,
            background: "#18181b",
            color: "#f4f4f5",
            padding: "10px 16px",
            borderRadius: 10,
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: 500,
            boxShadow: "0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
            border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
            zIndex: 9999,
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: 8,
          }}
        >
          <span>⚠️</span>
          <span>{toastMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      <UnsavedChangesModal
        isOpen={Boolean(unsavedModal?.isOpen)}
        pageName={unsavedModal?.pageName || ""}
        isSaving={isSavingUnsaved}
        onCancel={handleCancelNavigation}
        onDiscard={handleDiscardAndContinue}
        onSave={handleSaveAndContinue}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function App(): React.ReactElement {
  const [updateState, setUpdateState] = useState<{
    stage: "checking" | "available" | "downloading" | "downloaded" | "error" | "not-available" | null;
    progress?: number;
    error?: string;
  }>({ stage: null });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (window.electronAPI?.onUpdaterEvent) {
      const cleanup = window.electronAPI.onUpdaterEvent((data) => {
        setUpdateState((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          stage: data.stage as any,
          progress: data.progress,
          error: data.error,
        }));
      });
      return cleanup;
    }
  }, []);

  const shouldShowUpdater =
    updateState.stage === "downloading" ||
    updateState.stage === "downloaded";

  return (
    <>
      <AuthProvider>
        <ShopAdminShell />
      </AuthProvider>
      {shouldShowUpdater && (
        <UpdateScreen
          stage={updateState.stage as any}
          progress={updateState.progress}
          error={updateState.error}
          onRestart={() => window.electronAPI.restartApp()}
          onDismiss={() => setUpdateState({ stage: null })}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [24/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/context/AuthContext.tsx
PURPOSE: Authentication Context: Local Session, OTP Reset, Google OAuth
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useState,
  useEffect,
  useCallback,
} from "react";
import { getSupabase, sanitizeSubdomain, type ShopInfo } from "@printap/shared";

export type { ShopInfo };

export interface AuthContextType {
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  shopId: string | null;
  shop: ShopInfo | null;
  isLoading: boolean;
  isGoogleSigningIn: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  loginWithGoogle: () => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  cancelGoogleLogin: () => void;
  reopenGoogleBrowser: () => void;
  logout: () => void;
  refreshShop: () => Promise<void>;
  sendResetOtp: (email: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  resetPassword: (
    email: string,
    otp: string,
    newPassword: string
  ) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
}

const STORAGE_KEY = "printap_shop_id";
const SAVED_EMAIL_KEY = "printap_saved_email";
const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "https://www.mellod.in";

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}): React.ReactElement {
  const [shopId, setShopId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [shop, setShop] = useState<ShopInfo | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isGoogleSigningIn, setIsGoogleSigningIn] = useState(false);
  const activeGoogleSessionRef = React.useRef<{
    cancel: () => void;
    reopenBrowser: () => void;
  } | null>(null);

  const fetchShopDetails = useCallback(async (idOrSlug: string): Promise<ShopInfo | null> => {
    try {
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const isUuid =
        /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(
          idOrSlug
        );

      let query = supabase.from("shops").select("*");
      if (isUuid) {
        query = query.eq("id", idOrSlug);
      } else {
        query = query.eq("slug", idOrSlug.toLowerCase());
      }

      const { data, error } = await query.maybeSingle();

      if (error || !data) {
        // Fallback: match by sanitized name if slug is not set
        const { data: allActive } = await supabase
          .from("shops")
          .select("*")
          .eq("status", "active");

        if (allActive && allActive.length > 0) {
          const match = allActive.find(
            (s) => sanitizeSubdomain(s.name) === sanitizeSubdomain(idOrSlug)
          );
          if (match) {
            const resolved = match as ShopInfo;
            if (!resolved.slug) resolved.slug = sanitizeSubdomain(resolved.name);
            return resolved;
          }
        }

        console.warn("[Auth] Failed to load shop:", error?.message);
        return null;
      }

      const resolvedShop = data as ShopInfo;
      // Auto-ensure slug is always populated from shop name
      if (!resolvedShop.slug && resolvedShop.name) {
        resolvedShop.slug = sanitizeSubdomain(resolvedShop.name);
      }

      return resolvedShop;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[Auth] Error fetching shop details:", err);
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  // Restore saved shop from localStorage on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const initAuth = async () => {
      const savedId = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (savedId) {
        const shopDetails = await fetchShopDetails(savedId.trim());
        if (shopDetails && shopDetails.status !== "suspended") {
          setShopId(shopDetails.id);
          setShop(shopDetails);
        } else {
          localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        }
      }
      setIsLoading(false);
    };

    initAuth();
  }, [fetchShopDetails]);

  const login = async (
    identifier: string,
    password: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const cleanIdentifier = identifier.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!cleanIdentifier) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter your email or shop subdomain handle." };
    }
    if (!password) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter your password." };
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/login`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ identifier: cleanIdentifier, password }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        if (data.unregistered) {
          const onboardUrl = `${API_BASE_URL}/onboard`;
          if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
            window.electronAPI.openExternal(onboardUrl);
          } else {
            window.open(onboardUrl, "_blank");
          }
          return {
            success: false,
            error: "No registered shop found. Redirected to complete onboarding in your browser.",
          };
        }
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Authentication failed." };
      }

      const shopDetails = data.shop as ShopInfo;
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, shopDetails.id);
      setShopId(shopDetails.id);
      setShop(shopDetails);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Connection failed: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const sendResetOtp = async (
    email: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!cleanEmail || !cleanEmail.includes("@")) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter a valid email address." };
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/send-otp`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: cleanEmail, purpose: "reset_password" }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Failed to send reset code." };
      }
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Network error: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const resetPassword = async (
    email: string,
    otp: string,
    newPassword: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/reset-password`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
          otp: otp.trim(),
          newPassword,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Password reset failed." };
      }
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Network error: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const loginWithGoogle = async (): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const sessionId = crypto.randomUUID();
    setIsGoogleSigningIn(true);

    const authUrl = `${API_BASE_URL}/auth/desktop-login?session=${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}`;

    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      let settled = false;

      const cleanup = () => {
        if (channel) {
          try {
            channel.unsubscribe();
          } catch {}
        }
        if (pollTimer) clearInterval(pollTimer);
        if (deepLinkCleanup) deepLinkCleanup();
        setIsGoogleSigningIn(false);
        activeGoogleSessionRef.current = null;
      };

      const onSuccess = async (shopData: ShopInfo) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, shopData.id);
        setShopId(shopData.id);
        setShop(shopData);
        resolve({ success: true });
      };

      const onError = (error: string) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        resolve({ success: false, error });
      };

      // 1. Listen via Supabase Realtime Broadcast Channel
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const channel = supabase.channel(`desktop-auth:${sessionId}`, {
        config: { broadcast: { self: false } },
      });

      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "login-success" }, async ({ payload }) => {
        console.log("[Auth] Google login broadcast success:", payload);
        const p = payload as { shopId?: string; shop?: ShopInfo };
        if (p.shop) {
          onSuccess(p.shop);
        } else if (p.shopId) {
          const s = await fetchShopDetails(p.shopId);
          if (s) onSuccess(s);
          else onError("Failed to load shop details after login.");
        }
      });

      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "login-error" }, ({ payload }) => {
        const p = payload as { error?: string };
        onError(p.error || "Google authentication failed.");
      });

      channel.subscribe();

      // 2. Secondary Fallback: Poll desktop-session API every 1.5s
      const pollTimer = setInterval(async () => {
        if (settled) return;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/desktop-session?session=${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}`
          );
          if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.status === "authenticated" && data.shop) {
              onSuccess(data.shop as ShopInfo);
            } else if (data.status === "unregistered") {
              onError(
                data.error ||
                  "No registered shop found. Redirected to complete onboarding in your browser."
              );
            }
          }
        } catch {}
      }, 1500);

      // 3. Tertiary: Deep-link listener from Electron main process
      let deepLinkCleanup: (() => void) | null = null;
      if (window.electronAPI?.onDeepLinkAuth) {
        deepLinkCleanup = window.electronAPI.onDeepLinkAuth(
          async ({ sessionId: linkSession, shopId: linkShopId }) => {
            if (linkSession === sessionId && linkShopId) {
              const s = await fetchShopDetails(linkShopId);
              if (s) onSuccess(s);
            }
          }
        );
      }

      // Open external system browser
      if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
        window.electronAPI.openExternal(authUrl);
      } else {
        window.open(authUrl, "_blank");
      }

      // Store controller for cancellation or re-opening
      activeGoogleSessionRef.current = {
        cancel: () => {
          if (!settled) {
            settled = true;
            cleanup();
            resolve({ success: false, error: "Login cancelled by user." });
          }
        },
        reopenBrowser: () => {
          if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
            window.electronAPI.openExternal(authUrl);
          } else {
            window.open(authUrl, "_blank");
          }
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const cancelGoogleLogin = () => {
    activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.cancel();
  };

  const reopenGoogleBrowser = () => {
    activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.reopenBrowser();
  };

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    setShopId(null);
    setShop(null);
  };

  // Cancel active Google OAuth polling session on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.cancel();
    };
  }, []);

  const refreshShop = async () => {
    if (!shopId) return;
    const updated = await fetchShopDetails(shopId);
    if (updated) setShop(updated);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        isAuthenticated: !!shopId && !!shop,
        shopId,
        shop,
        isLoading,
        isGoogleSigningIn,
        login,
        loginWithGoogle,
        cancelGoogleLogin,
        reopenGoogleBrowser,
        logout,
        refreshShop,
        sendResetOtp,
        resetPassword,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextType {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider");
  }
  return context;
}


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [25/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/lib/shop-signaling.ts
PURPOSE: WebRTC Receiver & Signaling Channel: SDP Handshake, Chunk Assembler
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
 * Shop-side WebRTC signaling handler.
 *
 * Listens on the Supabase Realtime Broadcast channel for WebRTC offers
 * from customers. For each offer, creates a peer connection, generates
 * an answer, and relays ICE candidates back through the same channel.
 *
 * Supports both legacy single-file transfers and rich multi-file orders
 * with print settings, token codes, and pickup PINs.
 */

import {
  joinSignalingChannel,
  broadcast,
  DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
  waitForIceGathering,
  type SignalPayload,
  type PrintSettings,
  type TransferManifestOrder,
  type TransferFileHeaderMessage,
} from "@printap/shared";
import type { RealtimeChannel } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

export interface ShopSession {
  sessionId: string;
  pc: RTCPeerConnection;
  channel: RealtimeChannel;
  dataChannel: RTCDataChannel | null;
}

/** Active sessions keyed by sessionId */
const sessions = new Map<string, ShopSession>();

/** Early remote ICE candidates buffered before remoteDescription is set */
const earlyCandidates = new Map<string, RTCIceCandidateInit[]>();
const earlyCandidateTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
const sessionInactivityTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();

function resetSessionTimeout(sessionId: string, timeoutMs = 90_000): void {
  const existing = sessionInactivityTimers.get(sessionId);
  if (existing) clearTimeout(existing);
  sessionInactivityTimers.set(
    sessionId,
    setTimeout(() => {
      console.log(`[shop] Session ${sessionId} timed out due to inactivity`);
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }, timeoutMs)
  );
}

export interface ReceivedOrderFile {
  fileId: string;
  fileName: string;
  fileSize: number;
  pageCount: number;
  settings: PrintSettings;
  buffer: ArrayBuffer;
}

export interface ReceivedOrder {
  sessionId: string;
  order: TransferManifestOrder;
  files: ReceivedOrderFile[];
}

export type FileReceivedCallback = (
  sessionId: string,
  fileBuffer: ArrayBuffer,
  fileName?: string
) => void;

export type OrderReceivedCallback = (order: ReceivedOrder) => void;

export interface SignalingCallbacks {
  onSessionStart?: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onRequestToken?: (
    sessionId: string,
    hasColor: boolean,
    totalAmount?: number,
    fileCount?: number
  ) => void;
  onFileProgress?: (
    sessionId: string,
    fileName: string,
    pct: number,
    received: number,
    total: number,
    fileIndex?: number,
    totalFiles?: number
  ) => void;
  onFileReceived: FileReceivedCallback;
  onManifestReceived?: (sessionId: string, order: TransferManifestOrder) => void;
  onOrderReceived?: OrderReceivedCallback;
  onSessionError?: (sessionId: string, error: string) => void;
}

/**
 * Start listening for WebRTC offers on the given shop's signaling channel.
 */
export function startShopSignaling(
  shopId: string,
  callbacksOrHandler: FileReceivedCallback | SignalingCallbacks
): RealtimeChannel {
  const callbacks: SignalingCallbacks =
    typeof callbacksOrHandler === "function"
      ? { onFileReceived: callbacksOrHandler }
      : callbacksOrHandler;

  const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId);

  // Listen for new customer offers
  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "webrtc-offer" },
    async ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const { sessionId, data } = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      console.log(`[shop] Received offer for session ${sessionId}`);
      callbacks.onSessionStart?.(sessionId);
      await handleOffer(
        channel,
        sessionId,
        data as RTCSessionDescriptionInit,
        callbacks
      );
    }
  );

  // Listen for remote ICE candidates
  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "webrtc-ice" },
    async ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const { sessionId, data } = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      const candidateInit = data as RTCIceCandidateInit;
      if (!candidateInit || !candidateInit.candidate) return;
      const session = sessions.get(sessionId);

      // Buffer candidate if session doesn't exist yet or remote description isn't set
      if (!session || !session.pc.remoteDescription) {
        if (!earlyCandidates.has(sessionId)) {
          earlyCandidates.set(sessionId, []);
        }
        earlyCandidates.get(sessionId)!.push(candidateInit);
        if (!earlyCandidateTimers.has(sessionId)) {
          earlyCandidateTimers.set(
            sessionId,
            setTimeout(() => {
              earlyCandidates.delete(sessionId);
              earlyCandidateTimers.delete(sessionId);
              console.log(`[shop] Cleaned up abandoned early ICE candidates for ${sessionId}`);
            }, 60_000)
          );
        }
        console.log(`[shop] Buffered early ICE candidate for ${sessionId}`);
        return;
      }

      try {
        await session.pc.addIceCandidate(
          new RTCIceCandidate(candidateInit)
        );
        console.log(`[shop] Added remote ICE candidate for ${sessionId}`);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[shop] Failed to add ICE candidate:`, err);
      }
    }
  );

  // Listen for customer token allocation requests (zero-db local atomic tokens)
  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "request-token" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const { sessionId, hasColor, totalAmount, fileCount } =
        payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      console.log(
        `[shop] Received request-token for session ${sessionId} (hasColor: ${hasColor})`
      );
      callbacks.onRequestToken?.(sessionId, Boolean(hasColor), totalAmount, fileCount);
    }
  );

  console.log(`[shop] Signaling started for shop ${shopId}`);
  return channel;
}

/**
 * Stop signaling and cleanup active connections.
 */
export function stopShopSignaling(channel: RealtimeChannel | null): void {
  if (channel) {
    try {
      channel.unsubscribe();
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[shop] Error unsubscribing channel:", e);
    }
  }
  for (const timer of earlyCandidateTimers.values()) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
  earlyCandidateTimers.clear();
  earlyCandidates.clear();

  for (const timer of sessionInactivityTimers.values()) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
  sessionInactivityTimers.clear();

  for (const sessionId of Array.from(sessions.keys())) {
    cleanup(sessionId);
  }
}

/**
 * Handle an incoming WebRTC offer: create peer connection, answer, and
 * set up the DataChannel listener.
 */
async function handleOffer(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  sessionId: string,
  offer: RTCSessionDescriptionInit,
  callbacks: SignalingCallbacks
): Promise<void> {
  const existing = sessions.get(sessionId);
  if (existing) {
    console.log(
      `[shop] Session ${sessionId} already exists (state: ${existing.pc.connectionState}, signaling: ${existing.pc.signalingState})`
    );
    if (
      existing.pc.connectionState === "connected" ||
      existing.pc.connectionState === "connecting" ||
      existing.pc.signalingState === "stable"
    ) {
      if (existing.pc.localDescription) {
        console.log(`[shop] Re-transmitting existing answer for session ${sessionId}`);
        const localDesc = existing.pc.localDescription;
        await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-answer", {
          sessionId,
          data: { type: localDesc.type, sdp: localDesc.sdp },
        });
      }
      return;
    } else {
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }
  }

  resetSessionTimeout(sessionId);

  const pc = new RTCPeerConnection({
    iceServers: DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
    iceCandidatePoolSize: 10,
  });

  const session: ShopSession = {
    sessionId,
    pc,
    channel,
    dataChannel: null,
  };
  sessions.set(sessionId, session);

  // Relay our ICE candidates to the customer
  pc.onicecandidate = (event) => {
    if (event.candidate && event.candidate.candidate) {
      broadcast(channel, "webrtc-ice", {
        sessionId,
        data: event.candidate.toJSON(),
      });
    }
  };

  pc.onconnectionstatechange = () => {
    console.log(
      `[shop] Connection state: ${pc.connectionState} (ice: ${pc.iceConnectionState})`
    );
    if (pc.connectionState === "failed" || pc.connectionState === "closed") {
      callbacks.onSessionError?.(sessionId, "WebRTC peer connection closed or failed");
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }
  };

  // The customer creates the DataChannel; we listen for it here.
  pc.ondatachannel = (event) => {
    console.log(`[shop] DataChannel received: ${event.channel.label}`);
    session.dataChannel = event.channel;
    setupDataChannelReceiver(session, callbacks);
  };

  try {
    // Set remote offer and create answer
    await pc.setRemoteDescription(new RTCSessionDescription(offer));

    // Drain any buffered early ICE candidates now that remote description is set
    const buffered = earlyCandidates.get(sessionId);
    if (buffered && buffered.length > 0) {
      console.log(`[shop] Draining ${buffered.length} early ICE candidates for ${sessionId}`);
      for (const cand of buffered) {
        try {
          if (cand && cand.candidate) {
            await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(cand));
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn(`[shop] Failed to add early ICE candidate:`, e);
        }
      }
      earlyCandidates.delete(sessionId);
    }

    const answer = await pc.createAnswer();
    await pc.setLocalDescription(answer);

    // Wait up to 800ms for initial host (LAN) and STUN candidates to be gathered
    await waitForIceGathering(pc, 800);

    const localAnswer = pc.localDescription || answer;

    // Send answer back to customer via broadcast
    await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-answer", {
      sessionId,
      data: { type: localAnswer.type, sdp: localAnswer.sdp },
    });

    console.log(`[shop] Sent answer for session ${sessionId}`);
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    console.error(`[shop] Failed to negotiate answer:`, err);
    callbacks.onSessionError?.(sessionId, msg);
    cleanup(sessionId);
  }
}

/**
 * Set up the DataChannel to receive multi-file orders and file chunks.
 */
function setupDataChannelReceiver(
  session: ShopSession,
  callbacks: SignalingCallbacks
): void {
  const dc = session.dataChannel!;
  dc.binaryType = "arraybuffer";

  let manifest: TransferManifestOrder | null = null;
  const receivedFiles: ReceivedOrderFile[] = [];

  let currentFileHeader: TransferFileHeaderMessage | null = null;
  let chunks: ArrayBuffer[] = [];
  let expectedSize = 0;
  let receivedSize = 0;
  let legacyMetadata: { fileName: string; fileSize: number; totalChunks: number } | null = null;

  dc.onmessage = (event) => {
    resetSessionTimeout(session.sessionId);
    // String messages: JSON messages or EOF
    if (typeof event.data === "string") {
      // 1. Check for legacy plain EOF
      if (event.data === "EOF") {
        if (chunks.length > 0) {
          const assembled = new Uint8Array(receivedSize);
          let offset = 0;
          for (const chunk of chunks) {
            assembled.set(new Uint8Array(chunk), offset);
            offset += chunk.byteLength;
          }

          const fileName =
            currentFileHeader?.fileName ||
            legacyMetadata?.fileName ||
            `file_${receivedFiles.length + 1}`;

          if (currentFileHeader) {
            const alreadyPushed = receivedFiles.some(
              (f) => f.fileId === currentFileHeader!.fileId
            );
            if (!alreadyPushed) {
              receivedFiles.push({
                fileId: currentFileHeader.fileId,
                fileName: currentFileHeader.fileName,
                fileSize: currentFileHeader.fileSize,
                pageCount: currentFileHeader.pageCount,
                settings: currentFileHeader.settings,
                buffer: assembled.buffer,
              });
            }
          }

          // Trigger single-file callback ONLY if NOT part of a structured order manifest!
          if (!manifest) {
            callbacks.onFileReceived(session.sessionId, assembled.buffer, fileName);
          }

          chunks = [];
          receivedSize = 0;
          legacyMetadata = null;
        }
        return;
      }

      // 2. Parse JSON
      try {
        const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);

        // Case A: Order Manifest
        if (msg.type === "order-manifest") {
          console.log(`[shop] Order manifest received:`, msg);
          manifest = msg.order;
          callbacks.onManifestReceived?.(session.sessionId, msg.order);
          return;
        }

        // Case B: File Header
        if (msg.type === "file-header") {
          console.log(`[shop] File header received:`, msg);
          
          // SECURITY: Enforce maximum file size of 100MB to prevent Memory Exhaustion (DoS)
          if (msg.fileSize > 100 * 1024 * 1024) {
             callbacks.onSessionError?.(session.sessionId, "Security Exception: File size exceeds 100MB limit.");
             cleanup(session.sessionId);
             return;
          }

          currentFileHeader = msg as TransferFileHeaderMessage;
          expectedSize = currentFileHeader.fileSize;
          receivedSize = 0;
          chunks = [];
          callbacks.onFileProgress?.(
            session.sessionId,
            currentFileHeader.fileName,
            0,
            0,
            expectedSize,
            receivedFiles.length + 1,
            manifest?.totalFiles ?? 1
          );
          return;
        }

        // Case C: File EOF
        if (msg.type === "file-eof") {
          console.log(`[shop] File EOF received for ${msg.fileId}`);
          if (currentFileHeader && chunks.length > 0) {
            const assembled = new Uint8Array(receivedSize);
            let offset = 0;
            for (const chunk of chunks) {
              assembled.set(new Uint8Array(chunk), offset);
              offset += chunk.byteLength;
            }

            const alreadyPushed = receivedFiles.some(
              (f) => f.fileId === currentFileHeader!.fileId
            );
            if (!alreadyPushed) {
              receivedFiles.push({
                fileId: currentFileHeader.fileId,
                fileName: currentFileHeader.fileName,
                fileSize: currentFileHeader.fileSize,
                pageCount: currentFileHeader.pageCount,
                settings: currentFileHeader.settings,
                buffer: assembled.buffer,
              });
            }

            // Only call legacy onFileReceived if NOT part of a structured order manifest!
            if (!manifest) {
              callbacks.onFileReceived(
                session.sessionId,
                assembled.buffer,
                currentFileHeader.fileName
              );
            }
            chunks = [];
            receivedSize = 0;
          }
          return;
        }

        // Case D: Order Complete
        if (msg.type === "order-complete") {
          console.log(
            `[shop] Order complete received! Total files: ${receivedFiles.length}`
          );
          if (manifest && callbacks.onOrderReceived) {
            callbacks.onOrderReceived({
              sessionId: session.sessionId,
              order: manifest,
              files: receivedFiles,
            });
          }
          return;
        }

        // Case E: Legacy metadata { fileName, fileSize, totalChunks }
        if (msg.fileName && typeof msg.fileSize === "number") {
          // SECURITY: Enforce maximum file size of 100MB to prevent Memory Exhaustion (DoS)
          if (msg.fileSize > 100 * 1024 * 1024) {
             callbacks.onSessionError?.(session.sessionId, "Security Exception: File size exceeds 100MB limit.");
             cleanup(session.sessionId);
             return;
          }

          legacyMetadata = msg;
          expectedSize = msg.fileSize;
          receivedSize = 0;
          chunks = [];
          callbacks.onFileProgress?.(
            session.sessionId,
            msg.fileName,
            0,
            0,
            expectedSize,
            1,
            1
          );
          return;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[shop] Failed to parse message on DataChannel:`, err);
      }
      return;
    }

    // Binary message: file chunk
    const chunk = event.data as ArrayBuffer;
    chunks.push(chunk);
    receivedSize += chunk.byteLength;

    const currentName =
      currentFileHeader?.fileName ||
      legacyMetadata?.fileName ||
      "receiving...";

    if (expectedSize > 0) {
      const pct = Math.min(
        100,
        Math.round((receivedSize / expectedSize) * 100)
      );
      callbacks.onFileProgress?.(
        session.sessionId,
        currentName,
        pct,
        receivedSize,
        expectedSize,
        receivedFiles.length + 1,
        manifest?.totalFiles ?? 1
      );
    }
  };

  dc.onopen = () => {
    console.log(`[shop] DataChannel open for session ${session.sessionId}`);
  };

  dc.onclose = () => {
    console.log(`[shop] DataChannel closed for session ${session.sessionId}`);
    setTimeout(() => {
      cleanup(session.sessionId);
    }, 5000);
  };

  dc.onerror = (err) => {
    console.error(`[shop] DataChannel error:`, err);
    callbacks.onSessionError?.(
      session.sessionId,
      "DataChannel encountered an error"
    );
  };
}

/** Cleanup a completed or failed session. */
function cleanup(sessionId: string): void {
  const earlyTimer = earlyCandidateTimers.get(sessionId);
  if (earlyTimer) {
    clearTimeout(earlyTimer);
    earlyCandidateTimers.delete(sessionId);
  }
  earlyCandidates.delete(sessionId);

  const inactivityTimer = sessionInactivityTimers.get(sessionId);
  if (inactivityTimer) {
    clearTimeout(inactivityTimer);
    sessionInactivityTimers.delete(sessionId);
  }

  const session = sessions.get(sessionId);
  if (!session) return;

  try {
    session.dataChannel?.close();
    session.pc.close();
  } catch (e) {
    // Ignore close errors
  }
  sessions.delete(sessionId);
  console.log(`[shop] Session ${sessionId} cleaned up`);
}

/** Broadcast payment confirmation event to the customer. */
export async function broadcastPaymentConfirmed(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  sessionId: string,
  tokenCode?: string
): Promise<void> {
  await broadcast(channel, "payment-confirmed", {
    sessionId,
    tokenCode,
    confirmedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  });
}

/** Broadcast order/queue status to customer. */
export async function broadcastOrderStatus(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  payload: {
    sessionId: string;
    tokenCode?: string;
    queuePosition?: number;
    printerNumber?: number;
    paymentStatus?: string;
    status?: string;
    errorDetail?: string;
  }
): Promise<void> {
  await broadcast(channel, "order-status", payload);
}

/** Broadcast locally allocated authoritative token to customer. */
export async function broadcastTokenAssigned(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  payload: {
    sessionId: string;
    tokenCode: string;
    queuePosition?: number;
    printerNumber?: number;
  }
): Promise<void> {
  await broadcast(channel, "assign-token", payload);
}



───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [26/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/lib/shop-presence.ts
PURPOSE: Supabase Realtime Shop Presence & Heartbeat Engine
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import {
  getSupabase,
  presenceChannelName,
  setShopOnline,
  HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS,
} from "@printap/shared";
import type { RealtimeChannel } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

let currentChannel: RealtimeChannel | null = null;
let currentShopId: string | null = null;
let heartbeatTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
let isRunning = false;

export type PresenceConnectionStatus =
  | "disconnected"
  | "connecting"
  | "online"
  | "error";

type StatusListener = (status: PresenceConnectionStatus, detail?: string) => void;
const listeners = new Set<StatusListener>();

export function subscribePresenceStatus(listener: StatusListener): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

function notifyStatus(status: PresenceConnectionStatus, detail?: string) {
  for (const listener of listeners) {
    try {
      listener(status, detail);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[presence] Listener error:", e);
    }
  }
}

export function isPresenceActive(): boolean {
  return isRunning && currentChannel !== null;
}

export function getCurrentPresenceShopId(): string | null {
  return currentShopId;
}

/**
 * Starts the shop presence system:
 * 1. Sets is_online = true in Supabase database
 * 2. Joins the presence channel for this shop
 * 3. Tracks presence payload
 * 4. Schedules recurring 30s heartbeat to update DB and re-track
 * 5. Informs Electron main process of active shop ID
 */
export async function startPresence(
  shopId: string,
  onStatusChange?: StatusListener
): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  if (onStatusChange) {
    listeners.add(onStatusChange);
  }

  // If already running for this shop, return early
  if (isRunning && currentShopId === shopId && currentChannel) {
    return { success: true };
  }

  // If running for another shop, clean up first
  if (isRunning) {
    await stopPresence();
  }

  currentShopId = shopId;
  isRunning = true;
  notifyStatus("connecting", "Connecting to presence channel...");

  try {
    // Notify Electron main process
    if (window.electronAPI?.setActiveShopId) {
      window.electronAPI.setActiveShopId(shopId);
    }

    // 1. Mark online in database immediately
    const dbResult = await setShopOnline(shopId, true);
    if (!dbResult.success) {
      console.warn("[presence] Initial DB mark online warning:", dbResult.error);
    }

    // 2. Join Presence Channel
    const supabase = getSupabase();
    const chName = presenceChannelName(shopId);

    const channel = supabase.channel(chName, {
      config: {
        presence: {
          key: shopId,
        },
      },
    });

    currentChannel = channel;

    channel
      .on("presence", { event: "sync" }, () => {
        console.log(`[presence] Channel synced for shop ${shopId}`);
      })
      .subscribe(async (status) => {
        if (status === "SUBSCRIBED") {
          try {
            await channel.track({
              shopId,
              timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
              isOnline: true,
            });
            console.log(`[presence] Subscribed & tracking for shop ${shopId}`);
            notifyStatus("online", "Shop is online and broadcasting presence");
          } catch (trackErr) {
            console.error("[presence] Failed to track presence:", trackErr);
            notifyStatus("online", "Subscribed with tracking warning");
          }
        } else if (status === "CHANNEL_ERROR" || status === "TIMED_OUT") {
          console.error(`[presence] Channel status: ${status}`);
          notifyStatus("error", `Presence channel status: ${status}`);
        } else if (status === "CLOSED") {
          console.log(`[presence] Channel closed`);
        }
      });

    // 3. Start Heartbeat Loop (every 30s)
    if (heartbeatTimer) clearInterval(heartbeatTimer);
    heartbeatTimer = setInterval(async () => {
      if (!isRunning || !currentShopId) return;

      try {
        // Re-track presence in channel
        if (currentChannel) {
          await currentChannel.track({
            shopId: currentShopId,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            isOnline: true,
          });
        }

        // Heartbeat in DB (last_seen_at + is_online = true)
        await setShopOnline(currentShopId, true);
      } catch (hbErr) {
        console.warn("[presence] Heartbeat tick error:", hbErr);
      }
    }, HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS);

    return { success: true };
  } catch (err) {
    const errorMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    console.error("[presence] Failed to start presence:", err);
    isRunning = false;
    notifyStatus("error", errorMsg);
    return { success: false, error: errorMsg };
  }
}

/**
 * Stops the presence system:
 * 1. Clears heartbeat timer
 * 2. Untracks and leaves presence channel
 * 3. Sets is_online = false in DB
 * 4. Clears active shop in Electron main
 */
export async function stopPresence(): Promise<void> {
  const shopId = currentShopId;
  isRunning = false;
  currentShopId = null;

  if (heartbeatTimer) {
    clearInterval(heartbeatTimer);
    heartbeatTimer = null;
  }

  if (currentChannel) {
    try {
      await currentChannel.untrack();
      await currentChannel.unsubscribe();
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      supabase.removeChannel(currentChannel);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[presence] Error closing presence channel:", e);
    }
    currentChannel = null;
  }

  if (shopId) {
    try {
      if (window.electronAPI?.setActiveShopId) {
        window.electronAPI.setActiveShopId(null);
      }
      await setShopOnline(shopId, false);
      if (window.electronAPI?.setShopOffline) {
        await window.electronAPI.setShopOffline(shopId);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[presence] Error setting shop offline in DB:", e);
    }
  }

  notifyStatus("disconnected", "Shop is offline");
}


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [27/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/lib/fifo-dispatcher.ts
PURPOSE: FIFO Print Dispatcher: Queue Scheduler, Token Allocation, Hardware Spooler
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
 * FIFO Print Dispatcher & Local Atomic Token Allocator
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 * 1. 100% Local Atomic Token Allocation (zero database dependency, collision-free)
 * 2. FIFO Order Queue: Jobs are processed in the strict order they arrive
 * 3. Multi-Printer Capability Load Balancing:
 *    - Divides tasks across all active compatible printers (B&W vs. Color)
 *    - Allows concurrent printing across multiple physical printers
 *    - Falls back gracefully if printers are busy or disabled
 */

import type { PrintSettings } from "@printap/shared";
import type { PrinterAssignment } from "../components/PrintersControl";

export interface ReceivedJobFile {
  fileId?: string;
  fileName: string;
  fileSize: number;
  pageCount: number;
  settings: PrintSettings;
  buffer: ArrayBuffer;
}

export interface QueuedPrintJob {
  sessionId: string;
  tokenCode: string;
  printerNumber: number;
  targetPrinterName?: string;
  hasColor: boolean;
  files: ReceivedJobFile[];
  totalAmount: number;
  paymentMethod: string;
  paymentStatus: string;
  enqueuedAt: Date;
}

export interface PrinterWorkerStatus {
  printerName: string;
  printerNumber: number;
  role: "bw" | "color" | "both" | "disabled";
  isBusy: boolean;
  currentJobToken?: string;
  currentSessionId?: string;
  jobsCompleted: number;
}

export interface TokenAllocation {
  tokenCode: string;
  printerNumber: number;
  targetPrinterName?: string;
  queuePosition: number;
}

export interface DispatcherCallbacks {
  onJobStarted?: (job: QueuedPrintJob, printerName: string) => void;
  onJobFileProgress?: (
    sessionId: string,
    fileName: string,
    fileIndex: number,
    totalFiles: number,
    printerName: string
  ) => void;
  onJobCompleted?: (job: QueuedPrintJob, printerName: string) => void;
  onJobFailed?: (job: QueuedPrintJob, error: string, printerName: string) => void;
  onQueueUpdated?: (
    queuedJobs: QueuedPrintJob[],
    workers: PrinterWorkerStatus[]
  ) => void;
}

export class FifoPrintDispatcher {
  private queue: QueuedPrintJob[] = [];
  private allocatedTokens: Map<string, TokenAllocation> = new Map();
  private failedJobs: Map<string, QueuedPrintJob> = new Map();
  private printerWorkers: Map<
    string,
    {
      isBusy: boolean;
      currentJobToken?: string;
      currentSessionId?: string;
      jobsCompleted: number;
    }
  > = new Map();

  private dailySequence: number = 0;
  private currentDateStr: string = "";
  private shopId: string = "";
  private callbacks: DispatcherCallbacks = {};

  constructor(shopId: string, initialPrintsCount: number = 0) {
    this.shopId = shopId;
    this.initSequence(initialPrintsCount);
  }

  public setCallbacks(callbacks: DispatcherCallbacks): void {
    this.callbacks = callbacks;
  }

  public setShopId(shopId: string, initialPrintsCount: number = 0): void {
    this.shopId = shopId;
    this.initSequence(initialPrintsCount);
  }

  /**
   * Initializes local token sequence from date & localStorage or initial count.
   */
  private initSequence(initialCount: number = 0): void {
    const today = new Date().toDateString();
    this.currentDateStr = today;

    const storageKey = `printap_local_seq_${this.shopId || "default"}_${today}`;
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);

    if (saved !== null) {
      const parsed = parseInt(saved, 10);
      const higher = Number.isFinite(parsed)
        ? Math.max(parsed, initialCount, this.dailySequence)
        : Math.max(initialCount, this.dailySequence);
      this.dailySequence = higher;
    } else {
      this.dailySequence = Math.max(this.dailySequence, initialCount);
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, String(this.dailySequence));
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  /**
   * Synchronize the local sequence with the highest known sequence count
   * from the database and existing orders. Ensures we never jump backwards.
   */
  public syncSequence(highestKnownSequence: number): void {
    this.checkDayRollover();
    if (highestKnownSequence > this.dailySequence) {
      this.dailySequence = highestKnownSequence;
      const storageKey = `printap_local_seq_${this.shopId || "default"}_${this.currentDateStr}`;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, String(this.dailySequence));
      } catch {}
      console.log(
        `[FifoDispatcher] Synchronized daily token sequence forward to ${this.dailySequence}`
      );
    }
  }

  /**
   * Helper to parse and sync sequence numbers from today's order history.
   */
  public syncFromOrders(orders: Array<{ token_code?: string | null }>): void {
    let maxSeq = 0;
    for (const order of orders) {
      if (!order.token_code) continue;
      const clean = order.token_code.replace(/^#/, "");
      // Format #{printerNumber}{dailySequence}, e.g. "17" -> printer 1, sequence 7
      if (/^\d{2,}$/.test(clean)) {
        const seq = parseInt(clean.slice(1), 10);
        if (Number.isFinite(seq) && seq > maxSeq) {
          maxSeq = seq;
        }
      } else {
        const num = parseInt(clean, 10);
        if (Number.isFinite(num) && num > maxSeq) {
          maxSeq = num;
        }
      }
    }
    const targetSeq = maxSeq > 0 ? maxSeq : orders.length;
    this.syncSequence(targetSeq);
  }

  /**
   * Check and reset calendar day rollover.
   */
  private checkDayRollover(): void {
    const today = new Date().toDateString();
    if (today !== this.currentDateStr) {
      this.currentDateStr = today;
      this.dailySequence = 0;
      this.allocatedTokens.clear();
      const storageKey = `printap_local_seq_${this.shopId || "default"}_${today}`;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, "0");
      } catch {}
      console.log(`[FifoDispatcher] Day rolled over to ${today}, local token counter reset to 0`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Atomically allocate the next local token without any database call.
   * Format: #{printerNumber}{dailySequence} (e.g. #11, #22, #13)
   */
  public allocateToken(
    sessionId: string,
    hasColor: boolean,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): TokenAllocation {
    this.checkDayRollover();

    // If this session already has an allocated token, return it
    const existing = this.allocatedTokens.get(sessionId);
    if (existing) {
      return existing;
    }

    // Atomically increment local counter
    this.dailySequence += 1;
    const storageKey = `printap_local_seq_${this.shopId || "default"}_${this.currentDateStr}`;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(storageKey, String(this.dailySequence));
    } catch {
      // ignore localStorage quota errors
    }

    // Find compatible printer for the token prefix
    const compatiblePrinter = this.findBestCompatiblePrinter(hasColor, assignments);
    const printerNumber = compatiblePrinter?.printer_number || 1;
    const targetPrinterName = compatiblePrinter?.printer_name;

    const tokenCode = `#${printerNumber}${this.dailySequence}`;
    const queuePosition = this.getEstimatedQueuePosition();

    const allocation: TokenAllocation = {
      tokenCode,
      printerNumber,
      targetPrinterName,
      queuePosition,
    };

    this.allocatedTokens.set(sessionId, allocation);
    console.log(
      `[FifoDispatcher] Atomically allocated local token: ${tokenCode} (seq: ${this.dailySequence}) for session ${sessionId.slice(0, 8)}`
    );

    return allocation;
  }

  /**
   * Retrieve an already allocated token if present.
   */
  public getAllocatedToken(sessionId: string): TokenAllocation | undefined {
    return this.allocatedTokens.get(sessionId);
  }

  /**
   * Enqueue a job into the FIFO print queue.
   */
  public enqueue(
    job: QueuedPrintJob,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): void {
    // Avoid duplicate enqueues for the same session
    if (this.queue.some((q) => q.sessionId === job.sessionId)) {
      console.warn(`[FifoDispatcher] Session ${job.sessionId} already in queue`);
      return;
    }

    console.log(
      `[FifoDispatcher] Enqueued job: Token ${job.tokenCode}, ${job.files.length} files (hasColor: ${job.hasColor}). Queue depth: ${this.queue.length + 1}`
    );

    this.queue.push(job);
    this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);

    // Trigger FIFO processing immediately
    this.processQueue(assignments);
  }

  /**
   * Updates payment status of a job if it is currently in queue.
   */
  public updateJobPaymentStatus(
    sessionId: string, 
    paymentStatus: string,
    assignments?: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): void {
    const job = this.queue.find((q) => q.sessionId === sessionId);
    if (job) {
      job.paymentStatus = paymentStatus;
      if (assignments) {
        this.processQueue(assignments);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Cancels a pending job from the queue.
   */
  public cancelJob(sessionId: string, assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>): void {
    const index = this.queue.findIndex((q) => q.sessionId === sessionId);
    if (index !== -1) {
      this.queue.splice(index, 1);
      this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);
      console.log(`[FifoDispatcher] Cancelled job for session ${sessionId}`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Core FIFO Queue Processor with Multi-Printer capability matching and load balancing.
   */
  public processQueue(assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>): void {
    if (this.queue.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    // Iterate through queued jobs in FIFO order
    for (let i = 0; i < this.queue.length; i++) {
      const job = this.queue[i];

      // MUST confirm payment before printing
      if (job.paymentStatus !== "confirmed") {
        continue; // Skip this job and check the next one
      }

      // Find an available (idle) compatible printer
      const availablePrinter = this.findIdleCompatiblePrinter(job.hasColor, assignments);

      if (availablePrinter) {
        // Dequeue this job from the FIFO queue
        this.queue.splice(i, 1);
        i--; // Adjust index after splice

        const printerName = availablePrinter.printer_name;

        // Mark printer as busy
        const currentWorker = this.printerWorkers.get(printerName) || {
          isBusy: false,
          jobsCompleted: 0,
        };

        this.printerWorkers.set(printerName, {
          ...currentWorker,
          isBusy: true,
          currentSessionId: job.sessionId,
          currentJobToken: job.tokenCode,
        });

        this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);

        console.log(
          `[FifoDispatcher] Dispatching job ${job.tokenCode} to Printer #${availablePrinter.printer_number} (${printerName}).`
        );

        // Execute print asynchronously without blocking the loop so other printers can take other jobs!
        this.executePrintJob(job, availablePrinter, assignments);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Executes a print job on the designated physical printer.
   */
  private async executePrintJob(
    job: QueuedPrintJob,
    printer: PrinterAssignment,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): Promise<void> {
    const printerName = printer.printer_name;
    this.callbacks.onJobStarted?.(job, printerName);

    try {
      for (let fIdx = 0; fIdx < job.files.length; fIdx++) {
        const file = job.files[fIdx];
        const settings = file.settings;

        this.callbacks.onJobFileProgress?.(
          job.sessionId,
          file.fileName,
          fIdx + 1,
          job.files.length,
          printerName
        );

        // Send to Windows Print Spooler via Electron API
        const printRes = await (window as any).electronAPI.printPdf(file.buffer, job.sessionId, {
          printer: printerName,
          copies: settings.copies || 1,
          orientation: settings.orientation,
          scale: settings.pageFit === "fill" ? "noscale" : "fit",
        });

        if (printRes && !printRes.success) {
          throw new Error(printRes.error || `Hardware spooler error on ${printerName || "Default Printer"}`);
        }
      }

      console.log(
        `[FifoDispatcher] Successfully spooled & shredded Token ${job.tokenCode} on ${printerName || "Default Printer"}!`
      );

      // Clean up failedJobs if this was a retried job that succeeded
      this.failedJobs.delete(job.sessionId);

      // Increment completed count
      const worker = this.printerWorkers.get(printerName) || {
        isBusy: false,
        jobsCompleted: 0,
      };
      this.printerWorkers.set(printerName, {
        isBusy: false,
        currentJobToken: undefined,
        currentSessionId: undefined,
        jobsCompleted: worker.jobsCompleted + 1,
      });

      this.callbacks.onJobCompleted?.(job, printerName || "Default Printer");
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errorMsg = err?.message || String(err);
      console.error(
        `[FifoDispatcher] Error printing Token ${job.tokenCode} on ${printerName || "Default Printer"}:`,
        errorMsg
      );

      // Retain failed job in memory so shopkeeper can fix the printer and retry
      this.failedJobs.set(job.sessionId, job);

      const worker = this.printerWorkers.get(printerName) || {
        isBusy: false,
        jobsCompleted: 0,
      };
      this.printerWorkers.set(printerName, {
        isBusy: false,
        currentJobToken: undefined,
        currentSessionId: undefined,
        jobsCompleted: worker.jobsCompleted,
      });

      this.callbacks.onJobFailed?.(job, errorMsg, printerName || "Default Printer");
    } finally {
      this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);

      // Immediately process the next waiting job in the FIFO queue for this freed printer!
      setTimeout(() => {
        this.processQueue(assignments);
      }, 100);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Finds the best compatible idle printer.
   * If multiple idle printers are compatible, balances load by picking the one with fewest completed jobs.
   */
  private findIdleCompatiblePrinter(
    hasColor: boolean,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): PrinterAssignment | null {
    const list = Object.values(assignments).filter(
      (a) => a.role !== "disabled" && a.is_active !== false
    );

    // If no configured active assignments exist in the shop yet,
    // automatically provide a default fallback printer so jobs can print immediately!
    if (list.length === 0) {
      const fallbackPrinter: PrinterAssignment = {
        shop_id: this.shopId,
        printer_name: "", // empty string tells Windows spooler / ptp to use system default
        printer_number: 1,
        role: "both",
        is_default: true,
        is_active: true,
      };
      const workerState = this.printerWorkers.get("") || {
        isBusy: false,
        jobsCompleted: 0,
      };
      if (!workerState.isBusy) {
        return fallbackPrinter;
      }
      return null;
    }

    // Filter idle printers
    const idleList = list.filter((p) => {
      const state = this.printerWorkers.get(p.printer_name);
      return !state?.isBusy;
    });

    if (idleList.length === 0) return null;

    // Find compatible idle printers
    const compatibleIdle = idleList.filter((p) => this.isCompatible(p, hasColor));

    if (compatibleIdle.length === 0) return null;

    // Sort by best fit:
    // 1. Dedicated role preferred (B&W -> role 'bw'; Color -> role 'color')
    // 2. Fewest jobs completed (round-robin wear balancing)
    compatibleIdle.sort((a, b) => {
      const scoreA = this.getCompatibilityScore(a, hasColor);
      const scoreB = this.getCompatibilityScore(b, hasColor);
      if (scoreA !== scoreB) {
        return scoreB - scoreA;
      }
      const countA = this.printerWorkers.get(a.printer_name)?.jobsCompleted || 0;
      const countB = this.printerWorkers.get(b.printer_name)?.jobsCompleted || 0;
      return countA - countB;
    });

    return compatibleIdle[0];
  }

  /**
   * Finds the best compatible printer (even if currently busy) for token labeling and routing.
   */
  private findBestCompatiblePrinter(
    hasColor: boolean,
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): PrinterAssignment | null {
    const list = Object.values(assignments).filter(
      (a) => a.role !== "disabled" && a.is_active !== false
    );

    if (list.length === 0) {
      return {
        shop_id: this.shopId,
        printer_name: "",
        printer_number: 1,
        role: "both",
        is_default: true,
        is_active: true,
      };
    }

    const compatible = list.filter((p) => this.isCompatible(p, hasColor));
    if (compatible.length > 0) {
      compatible.sort((a, b) => {
        const scoreA = this.getCompatibilityScore(a, hasColor);
        const scoreB = this.getCompatibilityScore(b, hasColor);
        return scoreB - scoreA;
      });
      return compatible[0];
    }

    // Fallback: Default active printer or first active
    return list.find((a) => a.is_default) || list[0];
  }

  /**
   * Checks if printer capabilities match job requirements.
   */
  private isCompatible(printer: PrinterAssignment, hasColor: boolean): boolean {
    if (printer.role === "disabled" || printer.is_active === false) {
      return false;
    }
    if (hasColor) {
      return printer.role === "color" || printer.role === "both";
    } else {
      return printer.role === "bw" || printer.role === "both";
    }
  }

  /**
   * Scores compatibility (higher = better fit).
   * Dedicated B&W printer scores higher for B&W jobs to save color toner.
   * Dedicated Color printer scores higher for Color jobs.
   */
  private getCompatibilityScore(printer: PrinterAssignment, hasColor: boolean): number {
    if (hasColor) {
      if (printer.role === "color") return 100;
      if (printer.role === "both") return 50;
      return 0;
    } else {
      if (printer.role === "bw") return 100;
      if (printer.role === "both") return 50;
      return 0;
    }
  }

  private getEstimatedQueuePosition(): number {
    let busyCount = 0;
    for (const w of this.printerWorkers.values()) {
      if (w.isBusy) busyCount++;
    }
    return this.queue.length + (busyCount > 0 ? 1 : 0) + 1;
  }

  private notifyQueueUpdate(assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>): void {
    const list = Object.values(assignments).filter(
      (a) => a.role !== "disabled" && a.is_active !== false
    );

    const workers: PrinterWorkerStatus[] = list.map((p) => {
      const state = this.printerWorkers.get(p.printer_name);
      return {
        printerName: p.printer_name,
        printerNumber: p.printer_number,
        role: p.role,
        isBusy: Boolean(state?.isBusy),
        currentJobToken: state?.currentJobToken,
        currentSessionId: state?.currentSessionId,
        jobsCompleted: state?.jobsCompleted || 0,
      };
    });

    this.callbacks.onQueueUpdated?.([...this.queue], workers);
  }

  public getQueue(): QueuedPrintJob[] {
    return [...this.queue];
  }

  public getPrinterWorkerStatuses(
    assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>
  ): PrinterWorkerStatus[] {
    const list = Object.values(assignments).filter(
      (a) => a.role !== "disabled" && a.is_active !== false
    );

    return list.map((p) => {
      const state = this.printerWorkers.get(p.printer_name);
      return {
        printerName: p.printer_name,
        printerNumber: p.printer_number,
        role: p.role,
        isBusy: Boolean(state?.isBusy),
        currentJobToken: state?.currentJobToken,
        currentSessionId: state?.currentSessionId,
        jobsCompleted: state?.jobsCompleted || 0,
      };
    });
  }

  /**
   * Re-enqueues a failed job to the front of the queue to retry printing.
   */
  public retryJob(sessionId: string, assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>): boolean {
    const job = this.failedJobs.get(sessionId);
    if (!job) {
      console.warn(`[FifoDispatcher] Cannot retry: job ${sessionId} not found in failed jobs`);
      return false;
    }

    this.failedJobs.delete(sessionId);
    console.log(`[FifoDispatcher] Retrying failed job for Token ${job.tokenCode}...`);
    // Prepend to front of queue so retried job prints next (FIFO priority)
    this.queue.unshift(job);
    this.notifyQueueUpdate(assignments);
    this.processQueue(assignments);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Dismisses a failed job from the retry cache.
   */
  public dismissFailedJob(sessionId: string): void {
    this.failedJobs.delete(sessionId);
  }

  /**
   * Gets all currently failed jobs awaiting retry or dismissal.
   */
  public getFailedJobs(): QueuedPrintJob[] {
    return Array.from(this.failedJobs.values());
  }
}

PURPOSE: Navigation Sidebar & Live Online/Offline Toggle
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import {
  IconLive,
  IconPrinter,
  IconAnalytics,
  IconProfile,
  IconLogout,
  IconQr,
  IconTag,
  IconHistory,
  IconChevronLeft,
  IconCash,
} from "./Icons";

export type AdminPage = "live" | "history" | "printers" | "services" | "analytics" | "qr" | "tokens" | "profile";

interface SidebarProps {
  currentPage: AdminPage;
  onSelectPage: (page: AdminPage) => void;
  isOnline: boolean;
  onToggleOnline: (value: boolean) => void;
  isTogglingOnline?: boolean;
  onLogoutRequest?: () => void;
  onHideSidebar?: () => void;
  tokenBalance?: number | null;
  tokenAlert?: { balance: number; threshold: boolean } | null;
}

export const Sidebar: React.FC<SidebarProps> = ({
  currentPage,
  onSelectPage,
  isOnline,
  onToggleOnline,
  isTogglingOnline = false,
  onLogoutRequest,
  onHideSidebar,
  tokenBalance,
  tokenAlert,
}) => {
  const { shop, logout } = useAuth();
  const [showOfflineConfirm, setShowOfflineConfirm] = React.useState(false);

  const handleToggleClick = () => {
    if (isTogglingOnline) return;
    if (isOnline) {
      setShowOfflineConfirm(true);
    } else {
      onToggleOnline(true);
    }
  };

  const navItems: { id: AdminPage; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
    {
      id: "live",
      label: "Live Monitor",
      icon: <IconLive size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "history",
      label: "History",
      icon: <IconHistory size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "printers",
      label: "Printers Control",
      icon: <IconPrinter size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "services",
      label: "Services & Rates",
      icon: <IconTag size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "analytics",
      label: "Revenue & Analytics",
      icon: <IconAnalytics size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "qr",
      label: "Counter QR Stand",
      icon: <IconQr size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "tokens",
      label: "Token Top-up",
      icon: <IconCash size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
    {
      id: "profile",
      label: "Shop Profile",
      icon: <IconProfile size={18} className="sidebar-item-icon" />,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <aside className="sidebar">
        {/* Top Shop Header & Online/Offline Control */}
        <div className="sidebar-header">
          <div className="sidebar-top-shop">
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 6 }}>
              <h1 className="sidebar-top-shop-name" title={shop?.name || "Print Shop"}>
                {shop?.name || "Print Shop"}
              </h1>
              {onHideSidebar && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="sidebar-collapse-btn"
                  onClick={onHideSidebar}
                  title="Hide sidebar (Alt+S)"
                  aria-label="Hide sidebar"
                >
                  <IconChevronLeft size={15} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="sidebar-status-beacon">
              <span className={`beacon-dot ${isOnline ? "online" : "offline"}`} />
              <span>{isOnline ? "Terminal Online" : "Terminal Offline"}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Online / Offline Presence Toggle */}
          <div className={`sidebar-online-card ${isOnline ? "online" : "offline"}`}>
            <div className="sidebar-online-header">
              <div className="sidebar-online-label-group">
                <div className="sidebar-online-text">
                  <span className="sidebar-online-title">
                    {isOnline ? "Accepting Jobs" : "Shop Offline"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="sidebar-online-subtitle">
                    {isOnline ? "Customers can print" : "Website is offline"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                className={`toggle-switch-btn ${isOnline ? "checked" : ""} ${
                  isTogglingOnline ? "loading" : ""
                }`}
                onClick={handleToggleClick}
                disabled={isTogglingOnline}
                aria-label={isOnline ? "Go offline" : "Go online"}
                title={isOnline ? "Switch shop to offline" : "Switch shop to online"}
              >
                <span className="toggle-switch-knob">
                  {isTogglingOnline && <span className="toggle-spinner" />}
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Token Balance Card */}
          {tokenBalance !== undefined && tokenBalance !== null && (
            <div
              className={`sidebar-token-card ${
                tokenBalance === 0
                  ? "exhausted"
                  : tokenAlert
                  ? tokenAlert.balance <= 5
