                    ? "critical"
                    : "warning"
                  : "good"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="sidebar-token-header">
                <span className="sidebar-token-title">Token Balance</span>
                <span className="sidebar-token-count">{tokenBalance} left</span>
              </div>
              <div className="sidebar-token-bar-container">
                <div
                  className="sidebar-token-bar-fill"
                  style={{
                    width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (tokenBalance / 20) * 100))}%`, // Visual scale up to 20 for the bar
                  }}
                />
              </div>
              {tokenBalance === 0 ? (
                <div className="sidebar-token-alert-container">
                  <div className="sidebar-token-alert">
                    ⛔ Tokens exhausted. Top up to print.
                  </div>
                  <button type="button" className="sidebar-topup-btn" onClick={() => onSelectPage('tokens')}>Top Up</button>
                </div>
              ) : tokenAlert ? (
                <div className="sidebar-token-alert-container">
                  <div className="sidebar-token-alert">
                    {tokenAlert.balance <= 5 ? "🔴 Critical low balance!" : "🟡 Low balance — top up"}
                  </div>
                  <button type="button" className="sidebar-topup-btn" onClick={() => onSelectPage('tokens')}>Top Up</button>
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Clean Nav List */}
        <nav className="sidebar-nav">
          {navItems.map((item) => {
            const isActive = currentPage === item.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                className={`sidebar-item ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`}
                onClick={() => onSelectPage(item.id)}
              >
                {item.icon}
                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Footer Gateway */}
        <div className="sidebar-footer">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="sidebar-logout-btn"
            onClick={() => {
              if (onLogoutRequest) {
                onLogoutRequest();
              } else {
                logout();
              }
            }}
            title="Sign out of this shop terminal"
          >
            <span>Disconnect Terminal</span>
            <IconLogout size={13} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {showOfflineConfirm && (
        <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={() => setShowOfflineConfirm(false)}>
          <div
            className="confirm-dialog-card"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="confirm-dialog-title">Switch Shop to Offline?</div>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-body">
              Going offline will prevent new customers from uploading or printing from your website. Any orders already in progress will complete normally.
            </div>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-cancel"
                onClick={() => setShowOfflineConfirm(false)}
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-confirm"
                onClick={() => {
                  setShowOfflineConfirm(false);
                  onToggleOnline(false);
                }}
              >
                Go Offline
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
};


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [29/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/LiveDashboard.tsx
PURPOSE: Live Monitor: Active Print Progress, Live Queue, Handover Ledger
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { getShopCustomerUrl } from "@printap/shared";
import {
  IconLive,
  IconPrinter,
  IconQr,
  IconCopy,
  IconCheck,
  IconChevronDown,
  IconFileText,
  IconCash,
} from "./Icons";

export interface LiveJobFile {
  fileName: string;
  fileSize?: number;
  pageCount?: number;
  settings?: any;
}

export interface LiveJob {
  sessionId: string;
  fileName?: string;
  status: "receiving" | "saving" | "printing" | "shredding" | "done" | "error" | "cancelled";
  progressPct?: number;
  stage?: string;
  detail?: string;
  timestamp: Date;
  tokenCode?: string;
  pickupPin?: string;
  fileCount?: number;
  files?: LiveJobFile[];
  totalAmount?: number;
  paymentMethod?: string;
  paymentStatus?: "pending" | "confirmed" | "handed_over";
  paymentConfirmedAt?: Date;
}

import type { QueuedPrintJob } from "../lib/fifo-dispatcher";

interface LiveDashboardProps {
  currentJob: LiveJob | null;
  recentJobs: LiveJob[];
  pendingQueue: QueuedPrintJob[];
  todayPrintsCount: number;
  isListening: boolean;
  connectedPrintersCount: number;
  onConfirmPayment: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onCancelOrder: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onRetryJob?: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onDismissJob?: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onNavigateToHistory?: () => void;
  tokenBalance?: number | null;
  tokenAlert?: { balance: number; threshold: boolean } | null;
}

export const LiveDashboard: React.FC<LiveDashboardProps> = React.memo(({
  currentJob,
  recentJobs,
  pendingQueue,
  todayPrintsCount,
  isListening,
  connectedPrintersCount,
  onConfirmPayment,
  onCancelOrder,
  onRetryJob,
  onDismissJob,
  onNavigateToHistory,
  tokenBalance,
  tokenAlert,
}) => {
  const { shop } = useAuth();
  const [copiedLink, setCopiedLink] = useState(false);
  // Track open/close state for filenames dropdown per sessionId
  const [expandedJobs, setExpandedJobs] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const toggleJobExpanded = (sessionId: string) => {
    setExpandedJobs((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [sessionId]: !prev[sessionId],
    }));
  };

  const customerUrl = getShopCustomerUrl(shop, shop?.name || "shop");

  const handleCopyLink = () => {
    if (!customerUrl) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(customerUrl);
    setCopiedLink(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedLink(false), 2000);
  };

  const getStatusBadge = (status: LiveJob["status"]) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "receiving":
        return { label: "Receiving Stream", className: "job-status-dot active" };
      case "saving":
        return { label: "Verifying Memory", className: "job-status-dot pending" };
      case "printing":
        return { label: "Printing to Spooler", className: "job-status-dot active" };
      case "shredding":
        return { label: "Secure Shredding", className: "job-status-dot pending" };
      case "done":
        return { label: "Completed & Shredded", className: "job-status-dot success" };
      case "error":
        return { label: "Job Failed", className: "job-status-dot error" };
      case "cancelled":
        return { label: "Cancelled", className: "job-status-dot error" };
      default:
        return { label: status, className: "job-status-dot" };
    }
  };

  const stages = [
    { id: "stream", num: "1", label: "Receive Stream" },
    { id: "verify", num: "2", label: "Memory Check" },
    { id: "spool", num: "3", label: "Spool to Printer" },
    { id: "shred", num: "4", label: "Secure Shred" },
  ];

  const getStageIndex = (status: LiveJob["status"]) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "receiving": return 0;
      case "saving": return 1;
      case "printing": return 2;
      case "shredding": return 3;
      case "done": return 4;
      case "cancelled": return -1;
      default: return 0;
    }
  };

  const activeStageIdx = currentJob ? getStageIndex(currentJob.status) : 0;
  const currentPct = currentJob?.progressPct ?? 0;

  // Filter combined jobs list for payment side (active + recent)
  const allJobsMap = new Map<string, LiveJob>();

  // 1. Put recent jobs first
  recentJobs.forEach((j) => {
    if (j.sessionId) allJobsMap.set(j.sessionId, { ...j });
  });

  // 2. Merge currentJob if present
  if (currentJob && currentJob.sessionId) {
    const existing = allJobsMap.get(currentJob.sessionId);
    const isConfirmed =
      currentJob.paymentStatus === "confirmed" ||
      existing?.paymentStatus === "confirmed";

    allJobsMap.set(currentJob.sessionId, {
      ...existing,
      ...currentJob,
      tokenCode: currentJob.tokenCode || existing?.tokenCode,
      totalAmount:
        currentJob.totalAmount !== undefined && currentJob.totalAmount > 0
          ? currentJob.totalAmount
          : existing?.totalAmount,
      paymentMethod: currentJob.paymentMethod || existing?.paymentMethod || "upi",
      paymentStatus: isConfirmed
        ? "confirmed"
        : currentJob.paymentStatus || existing?.paymentStatus || "pending",
      paymentConfirmedAt:
        currentJob.paymentConfirmedAt || existing?.paymentConfirmedAt,
      files: currentJob.files || existing?.files,
      fileCount: currentJob.fileCount || existing?.fileCount,
    });
  }

  const allJobs = Array.from(allJobsMap.values());

  const pendingPayments = allJobs.filter(
    (j) =>
      // Exclude uninitialized socket connections that don't have order details yet
      (j.tokenCode || (j.totalAmount !== undefined && j.totalAmount > 0)) &&
      (!j.paymentStatus || j.paymentStatus === "pending")
  );
  const settledPayments = allJobs.filter(
    (j) => j.paymentStatus === "confirmed" || j.paymentStatus === "handed_over" || j.status === "done" || j.status === "cancelled"
  );
  // Only the last 10 are displayed on the live monitor; the rest are in History
  const displayedSettled = settledPayments.slice(0, 10);

  // Combine active job + recent jobs for print queue table
  const printQueueJobs = React.useMemo(() => {
    const list: LiveJob[] = [];
    if (currentJob) {
      list.push(currentJob);
    }
    recentJobs.forEach((rj) => {
      if (!currentJob || rj.sessionId !== currentJob.sessionId) {
        list.push(rj);
      }
    });
    return list;
  }, [currentJob, recentJobs]);

  return (
    <div className="content fade-in">
      {tokenBalance === 0 && (
        <div className="card" style={{ marginBottom: 20, backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)', borderColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2)' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>⛔</div>
            <div>
              <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: 'rgb(239, 68, 68)', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>Tokens Exhausted</h3>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13, marginTop: 4 }}>
                Your print shop is out of tokens. All incoming print jobs will be rejected. Please top up your tokens.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {tokenBalance !== 0 && tokenAlert && (
        <div className="card" style={{ marginBottom: 20, backgroundColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1)', borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2)' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>{tokenAlert.balance <= 5 ? '🔴' : '🟡'}</div>
            <div>
              <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: 'rgb(245, 158, 11)', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>Low Token Balance</h3>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13, marginTop: 4 }}>
                You have {tokenAlert.balance} tokens remaining. Top up soon to avoid interruption.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="page-header">
        <h1 className="page-title">
          <span>Live Monitor</span>
          <span className="status-badge active">Live & Connected</span>
        </h1>
        <p className="page-subtitle">
          Real-time print queue and counter payment verification for {shop?.name || "your counter"}.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Top Stat Cards (Terminal Gateway removed -> 3 balanced cards) */}
      <div className="stats-grid">
        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Today's Total Prints</span>
            <IconPrinter size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
          </div>
          <div className="stat-value">{todayPrintsCount}</div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">Direct peer-to-peer queue</div>
        </div>

        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Connected Printers</span>
            <IconPrinter size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
          </div>
          <div className="stat-value green">{connectedPrintersCount} Ready</div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">
            {connectedPrintersCount > 0 ? "Hardware detected & ready" : "No physical hardware detected"}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Pending Orders</span>
            <IconCash size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
          </div>
          <div
            className="stat-value"
            style={{
              color: pendingQueue.length > 0 ? "var(--accent-amber)" : "var(--accent-emerald)",
            }}
          >
            {pendingQueue.length}
          </div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">
            {pendingQueue.length > 0
              ? `${pendingQueue.length} awaiting action`
              : "All clear"}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
          TWO-PANEL TABLE SYSTEM:
          1. Live Orders (Unified Print Queue & Action Required)
          2. Settled Orders (Done Payments - Table)
      ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <div className="live-monitor-tables-grid">
        {/* ─── TABLE 1: Live Orders (Unified Print Queue & Action Required) ─── */}
        <div className="monitor-table-card">
          <div className="monitor-table-header">
            <div className="monitor-table-title">
              <IconPrinter size={16} color="var(--text-primary)" />
              <span>Live Orders</span>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "8px" }}>
              {pendingQueue.length > 0 && (
                <span className="status-badge" style={{ background: "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)", color: "#b45309" }}>
                  {pendingQueue.length} Action Required
                </span>
              )}
              <span className="status-badge active">
                {currentJob ? "1 Processing" : `${printQueueJobs.length} Today`}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="monitor-table-container">
            <table className="monitor-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th style={{ width: 68 }}>Token</th>
                  <th>Document & Specs</th>
                  <th>Amount</th>
                  <th style={{ width: 140, textAlign: "right" }}>Status / Action</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {printQueueJobs.length > 0 || pendingQueue.length > 0 ? (
                  Array.from(new Set([...printQueueJobs, ...pendingQueue])).map((rawJob) => {
                    const job = rawJob as any;
                    const isActive = currentJob?.sessionId === job.sessionId;
                    const isPendingPayment = !job.paymentStatus || job.paymentStatus === "pending";
                    const docCount = job.files?.length || job.fileCount || 1;
                    const amount = job.totalAmount ?? 0;
                    const totalPages =
                      job.files && job.files.length > 0
                        ? job.files.reduce(
                            (sum: number, f: LiveJobFile) => sum + (f.pageCount || 1) * (f.settings?.copies || 1),
                            0
                          )
                        : 1;
                    const isExpanded = !!expandedJobs[job.sessionId];
                    const hasMultiFiles = job.files && job.files.length > 1;

                    return (
                      <React.Fragment key={job.sessionId}>
                        <tr className={isActive || isPendingPayment ? "table-row-active" : ""}>
                          <td>
                            <span className="token-pill">{job.tokenCode || "#--"}</span>
                          </td>
                          <td>
                            <div className="table-doc-cell">
                              <span className="table-file-title" title={job.fileName || "Customer Document"}>
                                {job.fileName || "Customer Document"}
                              </span>
                              <div className="table-file-subtext">
                                <span>
                                  {docCount} {docCount === 1 ? "doc" : "docs"} • {totalPages} {totalPages === 1 ? "pg" : "pgs"}
                                </span>
                                {hasMultiFiles && (
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    className={`table-expand-btn ${isExpanded ? "open" : ""}`}
                                    onClick={() => toggleJobExpanded(job.sessionId)}
                                    title="View files"
                                  >
                                    <span>{isExpanded ? "Hide" : "Files"}</span>
                                    <IconChevronDown size={11} />
                                  </button>
                                )}
                              </div>
                              {isActive && job.status === "receiving" && typeof job.progressPct === "number" && (
                                <div className="table-progress-track">
                                  <div
                                    className="table-progress-fill"
                                    style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, job.progressPct))}%` }}
                                  />
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </td>
                          <td>
                            <div className="table-amount-due" style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                              ₹{amount.toFixed(2)}
                            </div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: "10px", color: "var(--text-muted)", marginTop: "2px", fontWeight: 500 }}>
                              {job.paymentMethod?.toUpperCase() || "UPI"}
                            </div>
                          </td>
                          <td style={{ textAlign: "right" }}>
                            {isPendingPayment ? (
                              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "6px", justifyContent: "flex-end", alignItems: "center" }}>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  className="table-confirm-btn hover-pop"
                                  style={{ padding: "6px 12px", background: "var(--accent-emerald)", color: "white", borderRadius: "6px", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: "11px", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", boxShadow: "0 2px 4px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2)" }}
                                  onClick={() => onConfirmPayment(job.sessionId)}
                                  title={`Confirm Payment & Print (₹${amount.toFixed(2)})`}
                                >
                                  Print
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  className="btn-dismiss-tiny hover-pop"
                                  style={{ padding: "6px", borderRadius: "6px", background: "#fee2e2", color: "#dc2626", border: "none", cursor: "pointer" }}
                                  onClick={() => onCancelOrder(job.sessionId)}
                                  title="Cancel Order & Refund"
                                >
                                  ✕
                                </button>
                              </div>
                            ) : job.status === "receiving" ? (
                              <span className="status-badge" style={{ background: "#e0f2fe", color: "#0369a1" }}>
                                Stream ({job.progressPct || 0}%)
                              </span>
                            ) : job.status === "printing" ? (
                              <span className="status-badge active" style={{ background: "#fef3c7", color: "#b45309" }}>
                                ● Printing
                              </span>
                            ) : job.status === "error" ? (
                              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 3, alignItems: "flex-end" }}>
                                <span className="status-badge" style={{ background: "#fee2e2", color: "#dc2626" }}>
                                  Failed
                                </span>
                                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 3 }}>
                                  <button type="button" className="btn-retry-tiny" onClick={() => onRetryJob?.(job.sessionId)}>Retry</button>
                                  <button type="button" className="btn-dismiss-tiny" onClick={() => onDismissJob?.(job.sessionId)}>✕</button>
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="status-badge" style={{ background: "#ecfdf5", color: "#047857" }}>
                                ✓ Spooled
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </td>
                        </tr>

                        {/* Collapsible files row if expanded */}
                        {isExpanded && job.files && job.files.length > 0 && (
                          <tr className="table-expanded-row">
                            <td colSpan={4}>
                              <div className="table-expanded-files-list">
                                {job.files.map((file: LiveJobFile, fIdx: number) => (
                                  <div key={fIdx} className="table-expanded-file-row">
                                    <IconFileText size={12} color="var(--text-muted)" />
                                    <span className="file-name" title={file.fileName}>
                                      {file.fileName}
                                    </span>
                                    <span className="file-meta">
                                      {file.pageCount || 1} pg • {(file.settings?.colorMode || "bw").toUpperCase()} •{" "}
                                      {file.settings?.copies || 1} cp
                                    </span>
                                  </div>
                                ))}
                              </div>
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        )}
                      </React.Fragment>
                    );
                  })
                ) : (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={4} className="monitor-table-empty">
                      <div className="empty-state-content">
                        <IconPrinter size={26} color="var(--text-muted)" />
                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-secondary)", marginTop: 6 }}>
                          Queue is idle
                        </div>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", margin: "3px 0 0" }}>
                          New orders will appear here automatically.
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ─── TABLE 3: Done Payments (Towards Right of Payment Counter) ─── */}
        <div className="monitor-table-card">
          <div className="monitor-table-header">
            <div className="monitor-table-title">
              <IconCheck size={16} color="var(--accent-emerald)" />
              <span>Done Payments</span>
            </div>
            <span className="status-badge" style={{ background: "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)", color: "#059669" }}>
              {settledPayments.length} Settled
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="monitor-table-container">
            <table className="monitor-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th style={{ width: 68 }}>Token</th>
                  <th>Received</th>
                  <th>Method & Time</th>
                  <th style={{ width: 85, textAlign: "right" }}>Status</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {displayedSettled.length > 0 ? (
                  displayedSettled.map((job) => {
                    const amount = job.totalAmount ?? 0;

                    return (
                      <tr key={job.sessionId}>
                        <td>
                          <span className="token-pill">{job.tokenCode || "#--"}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <span className="table-amount-settled">₹{amount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <div className="table-method-cell">
                            <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-secondary)" }}>
                              {job.paymentMethod ? job.paymentMethod.toUpperCase() : "UPI"}
                            </span>
                            <span className="subtext">
                              {job.timestamp.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td style={{ textAlign: "right" }}>
                          {job.status === "cancelled" ? (
                             <span className="status-badge" style={{ background: "#fee2e2", color: "#dc2626" }}>Cancelled</span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="table-verified-badge">
                              <IconCheck size={12} />
                              <span>Done</span>
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })
                ) : (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={4} className="monitor-table-empty">
                      <div className="empty-state-content">
                        <IconCash size={26} color="var(--text-muted)" />
                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-secondary)", marginTop: 6 }}>
                          No settled orders yet
                        </div>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", margin: "3px 0 0" }}>
                          Completed orders will be logged here.
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          {settledPayments.length > 7 && onNavigateToHistory && (
            <div className="monitor-table-footer">
              <button type="button" onClick={onNavigateToHistory} className="table-history-link">
                <span>View all {settledPayments.length} in History →</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
});


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [30/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/PrintHistory.tsx
PURPOSE: Print History & Activity Ledger with CSV Export
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { getSupabase } from "@printap/shared";
import { IconHistory, IconRefresh, IconFileText, IconCheck, IconCash } from "./Icons";
import type { LiveJob } from "./LiveDashboard";

export type HistoryDateFilter = "all_time" | "today" | "yesterday" | "this_week" | "this_month";

export interface PrintOrderRecord {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  session_id: string;
  token_code: string | null;
  payment_method: "upi" | "cash" | string | null;
  payment_status: string;
  total_amount: number;
  status: string;
  created_at: string;
  file_count?: number;
}

interface PrintHistoryProps {
  recentJobs?: LiveJob[];
}

export const PrintHistory: React.FC<PrintHistoryProps> = React.memo(({ recentJobs = [] }) => {
  const { shopId, shop } = useAuth();
  const [dateFilter, setDateFilter] = useState<HistoryDateFilter>("all_time");
  const [methodFilter, setMethodFilter] = useState<"all" | "upi" | "cash">("all");
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<PrintOrderRecord[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchOrders = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!shopId) return;
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("print_orders")
        .select("id, shop_id, session_id, token_code, payment_method, payment_status, total_amount, status, created_at")
        .eq("shop_id", shopId)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      if (!error && data) {
        setOrders(data as PrintOrderRecord[]);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[PrintHistory] Error loading print history:", err);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [shopId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchOrders();
  }, [fetchOrders]);

  const handleCopy = (text: string, id: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedId(id);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(null), 2000);
  };

  // Combine fetched orders with in-memory recent jobs (avoid duplicates by session_id)
  const allOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, PrintOrderRecord>();

    // 1. Add DB orders
    orders.forEach((o) => {
      map.set(o.session_id, o);
    });

    // 2. Overlay recent jobs
    recentJobs.forEach((j) => {
      if (!j.sessionId) return;
      const existing = map.get(j.sessionId);
      if (existing) {
        map.set(j.sessionId, {
          ...existing,
          token_code: j.tokenCode || existing.token_code,
          total_amount: j.totalAmount !== undefined && j.totalAmount > 0 ? j.totalAmount : existing.total_amount,
          payment_status:
            j.paymentStatus === "confirmed" || existing.payment_status === "confirmed"
              ? "confirmed"
              : j.paymentStatus || existing.payment_status,
          file_count: j.files?.length || j.fileCount || existing.file_count,
        });
      } else {
        map.set(j.sessionId, {
          id: j.sessionId,
          shop_id: shopId || "",
          session_id: j.sessionId,
          token_code: j.tokenCode || null,
          payment_method: j.paymentMethod || "upi",
          payment_status: j.paymentStatus || "pending",
          total_amount: j.totalAmount || 0,
          status: j.status || "done",
          created_at: (j.timestamp || new Date()).toISOString(),
          file_count: j.files?.length || j.fileCount || 1,
        });
      }
    });

    return Array.from(map.values()).sort(
      (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
    );
  }, [orders, recentJobs, shopId]);

  // Filter orders by date, payment method, and search
  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const startOfToday = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
    const startOfYesterday = new Date(startOfToday.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const endOfYesterday = startOfToday;
    const startOfWeek = new Date(startOfToday.getTime() - 6 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const startOfMonth = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);

    return allOrders.filter((order) => {
      const orderDate = new Date(order.created_at);

      // Date range filtering
      if (dateFilter === "today") {
        if (orderDate < startOfToday) return false;
      } else if (dateFilter === "yesterday") {
        if (orderDate < startOfYesterday || orderDate >= endOfYesterday) return false;
      } else if (dateFilter === "this_week") {
        if (orderDate < startOfWeek) return false;
      } else if (dateFilter === "this_month") {
        if (orderDate < startOfMonth) return false;
      }

      // Payment method filtering
      if (methodFilter !== "all") {
        if (order.payment_method?.toLowerCase() !== methodFilter) return false;
      }

      // Text search filtering (Token Code or Session ID)
      if (searchQuery.trim()) {
        const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
        const matchesToken = (order.token_code || "").toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchesSession = (order.session_id || "").toLowerCase().includes(q);
        if (!matchesToken && !matchesSession) return false;
      }

      return true;
    });
  }, [allOrders, dateFilter, methodFilter, searchQuery]);

  // Aggregate stats
  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    let totalRevenue = 0;
    let upiRevenue = 0;
    let cashRevenue = 0;

    filteredOrders.forEach((o) => {
      const amt = Number(o.total_amount) || 0;
      totalRevenue += amt;
      if (o.payment_method === "upi") upiRevenue += amt;
      else if (o.payment_method === "cash") cashRevenue += amt;
    });

    return {
      count: filteredOrders.length,
      totalRevenue,
      upiRevenue,
      cashRevenue,
    };
  }, [filteredOrders]);

  return (
    <div className="content fade-in" style={{ paddingBottom: 40 }}>
      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="page-header" style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "flex-start", justifyContent: "space-between", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 16 }}>
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            <IconHistory size={24} style={{ color: "var(--text-primary)" }} />
            <span>Print History</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Complete archive of all printed documents, tokens, and verified payments for {shop?.name || "your counter"}.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={fetchOrders}
          disabled={isLoading}
          className="btn-secondary"
          style={{
            display: "inline-flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: 8,
            padding: "8px 14px",
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: 600,
            cursor: "pointer",
          }}
          title="Refresh orders from database"
        >
          <IconRefresh size={15} className={isLoading ? "spin" : ""} />
          <span>{isLoading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Summary Stat Cards */}
      <div className="stats-grid" style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Total Prints Filtered</span>
            <IconFileText size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
          </div>
          <div className="stat-value">{stats.count}</div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">Customer print orders</div>
        </div>

        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Revenue Collected</span>
            <IconCash size={16} color="var(--accent-emerald)" />
          </div>
          <div className="stat-value" style={{ color: "var(--accent-emerald)" }}>
            ₹{stats.totalRevenue.toFixed(2)}
          </div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">
            UPI: ₹{stats.upiRevenue.toFixed(2)} • Cash: ₹{stats.cashRevenue.toFixed(2)}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-label">
            <span>Average Order Value</span>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>₹/order</span>
          </div>
          <div className="stat-value">
            ₹{stats.count > 0 ? (stats.totalRevenue / stats.count).toFixed(2) : "0.00"}
          </div>
          <div className="stat-footnote">Per session average</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filter and Search Bar */}
      <div
        className="card"
        style={{
          padding: "14px 18px",
          marginBottom: 16,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          flexWrap: "wrap",
          gap: 12,
        }}
      >
        {/* Left: Date Presets */}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          {(
            [
              { id: "all_time", label: "All Time" },
              { id: "today", label: "Today" },
              { id: "yesterday", label: "Yesterday" },
              { id: "this_week", label: "Last 7 Days" },
              { id: "this_month", label: "This Month" },
            ] as const
          ).map((preset) => (
            <button
              key={preset.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setDateFilter(preset.id)}
              className={`pill-btn ${dateFilter === preset.id ? "active" : ""}`}
              style={{
                padding: "6px 12px",
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm)",
                border: dateFilter === preset.id ? "1px solid #16181D" : "1px solid var(--border-subtle)",
                background: dateFilter === preset.id ? "#16181D" : "transparent",
                color: dateFilter === preset.id ? "#ffffff" : "var(--text-secondary)",
                cursor: "pointer",
                transition: "var(--transition)",
              }}
            >
              {preset.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Right: Method Filter & Search Box */}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          {/* Method toggle */}
          <div style={{ display: "inline-flex", borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm)", background: "rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.04)", border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle)", padding: 2 }}>
            {(
              [
                { id: "all", label: "All" },
                { id: "upi", label: "UPI" },
                { id: "cash", label: "Cash" },
              ] as const
            ).map((m) => (
              <button
                key={m.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setMethodFilter(m.id)}
                style={{
                  padding: "4px 10px",
                  fontSize: 11,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm)",
                  border: "none",
                  background: methodFilter === m.id ? "#16181D" : "transparent",
                  color: methodFilter === m.id ? "#ffffff" : "var(--text-secondary)",
                  boxShadow: methodFilter === m.id ? "var(--shadow-sm)" : "none",
                  cursor: "pointer",
                }}
              >
                {m.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Search box */}
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search Token (e.g. #214)..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            style={{
              padding: "6px 12px",
              fontSize: 12,
              borderRadius: "var(--radius-md)",
              border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle)",
              background: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8)",
              color: "var(--text-primary)",
              outline: "none",
              minWidth: 180,
            }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Orders List / Table */}
      <div className="card" style={{ padding: 0, overflow: "hidden" }}>
        <div
          style={{
            padding: "12px 18px",
            borderBottom: "1px solid var(--border-subtle)",
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "space-between",
            background: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.01)",
          }}
        >
          <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
            Archived Print Orders ({filteredOrders.length})
          </span>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
            Sorted by newest first
          </span>
        </div>

        {filteredOrders.length > 0 ? (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column" }}>
            {filteredOrders.map((order, idx) => {
              const dateObj = new Date(order.created_at);
              const formattedDate = dateObj.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                month: "short",
                day: "numeric",
                year: "numeric",
              });
              const formattedTime = dateObj.toLocaleTimeString([], {
                hour: "2-digit",
                minute: "2-digit",
              });
              const amount = Number(order.total_amount) || 0;
              const isCopied = copiedId === order.session_id;

              return (
                <div
                  key={order.session_id || order.id || idx}
                  style={{
                    display: "flex",
                    alignItems: "center",
                    justifyContent: "space-between",
                    padding: "14px 18px",
                    borderBottom: idx < filteredOrders.length - 1 ? "1px solid var(--border-subtle)" : "none",
                    gap: 12,
                    flexWrap: "wrap",
                    transition: "var(--transition)",
                  }}
                  onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                    e.currentTarget.style.background = "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.02)";
                  }}
                  onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                    e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent";
                  }}
                >
                  {/* Left: Token badge & date */}
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, minWidth: 200 }}>
                    <span
                      className="token-pill"
                      style={{
                        fontSize: 14,
                        fontWeight: 800,
                        padding: "5px 12px",
                        letterSpacing: "0.02em",
                      }}
                    >
                      {order.token_code || "#--"}
                    </span>

                    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                        {formattedDate} • {formattedTime}
                      </span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
                        Session: {order.session_id ? order.session_id.slice(0, 8) + "..." : "Local"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Middle: Method badge & Status */}
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
                    <span
                      style={{
                        padding: "3px 8px",
                        borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm)",
                        fontSize: 11,
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        background:
                          order.payment_method === "upi"
                            ? "rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.1)"
                            : "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1)",
                        color:
                          order.payment_method === "upi"
                            ? "#4f46e5"
                            : "#b45309",
                        textTransform: "uppercase",
                      }}
                    >
                      {order.payment_method ? order.payment_method.toUpperCase() : "UPI"}
                    </span>

                    <span
                      style={{
                        display: "inline-flex",
                        alignItems: "center",
                        gap: 4,
                        fontSize: 11,
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        color: "#059669",
                        background: "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1)",
                        padding: "3px 8px",
                        borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm)",
                      }}
                    >
                      <IconCheck size={12} />
                      <span>Settled & Paid</span>
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {/* Right: Amount & Copy */}
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 14 }}>
                    <div style={{ textAlign: "right" }}>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 800, color: "#16181D", fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)" }}>
                        ₹{amount.toFixed(2)}
                      </div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
                        Verified
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleCopy(order.token_code || order.session_id, order.session_id)}
                      style={{
                        padding: "6px 10px",
                        borderRadius: "var(--radius-md)",
                        border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle)",
                        background: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03)",
                        color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                        fontSize: 11,
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        cursor: "pointer",
                      }}
                      title="Copy Token Code"
                    >
                      {isCopied ? "Copied ✓" : "Copy"}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ padding: "48px 24px", textAlign: "center", color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
            <div
              style={{
                width: 44,
                height: 44,
                borderRadius: "50%",
                background: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04)",
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "center",
                margin: "0 auto 12px",
                color: "var(--text-muted)",
              }}
            >
              <IconHistory size={22} />
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-secondary)" }}>
              No Print Orders Found
            </div>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--text-muted)", marginTop: 4 }}>
              {searchQuery || dateFilter !== "all_time" || methodFilter !== "all"
                ? "Try adjusting your search query or date filters."
                : "Completed print orders will automatically appear in this archive."}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
});


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [31/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/PrintersControl.tsx
PURPOSE: Hardware Printer Mapping & Capability Assignment
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { getSupabase } from "@printap/shared";
import {
  IconPrinter,
  IconUsb,
  IconNetwork,
  IconWifi,
  IconRefresh,
  IconCheck,
} from "./Icons";

export type PrinterRole = "bw" | "color" | "both" | "disabled";

import type { DirtyHandler } from "./ServicesRates";

export interface PrinterAssignment {
  id?: string;
  shop_id: string;
  printer_name: string;
  printer_number: number;
  role: PrinterRole;
  is_default: boolean;
  is_active: boolean;
}

export interface PrintersControlProps {
  printers?: PrinterInfo[];
  isLoadingPrinters?: boolean;
  onRescanPrinters?: () => Promise<void>;
  onAssignmentsSaved?: (assignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment>) => void;
  onRegisterDirtyHandler?: (handler: DirtyHandler | null) => void;
}

export const PrintersControl: React.FC<PrintersControlProps> = React.memo(({
  printers: externalPrinters,
  isLoadingPrinters: externalIsLoading,
  onRescanPrinters,
  onAssignmentsSaved,
  onRegisterDirtyHandler,
}) => {
  const { shopId } = useAuth();
  const [internalPrinters, setInternalPrinters] = useState<PrinterInfo[]>([]);
  const [internalIsLoading, setInternalIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<Record<string, PrinterAssignment>>({});
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState<{
    text: string;
    type: "success" | "error";
  } | null>(null);

  // Initial snapshot to track unsaved edits
  const initialAssignmentsRef = React.useRef<string>("");

  const printers = externalPrinters !== undefined ? externalPrinters : internalPrinters;
  const isLoading = externalIsLoading !== undefined ? externalIsLoading : internalIsLoading;

  const getStorageKey = useCallback(
    () => `printap_printers_${shopId || "default"}`,
    [shopId]
  );

  // Load assignments from Supabase or localStorage
  const loadAssignments = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!shopId) return;

    try {
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("printer_assignments")
        .select("*")
        .eq("shop_id", shopId);

      if (!error && data && data.length > 0) {
        const map: Record<string, PrinterAssignment> = {};
        data.forEach((item, idx) => {
          map[item.printer_name] = {
            ...item,
            printer_number: item.printer_number || (idx + 1),
          };
        });
        setAssignments(map);
        initialAssignmentsRef.current = JSON.stringify(map);
        localStorage.setItem(getStorageKey(), JSON.stringify(map));
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // Supabase table not created yet, fall back to local storage
    }

    const cached = localStorage.getItem(getStorageKey());
    if (cached) {
      try {
        const map = JSON.parse(cached);
        Object.keys(map).forEach((k, idx) => {
          if (!map[k].printer_number) {
            map[k].printer_number = idx + 1;
          }
        });
        setAssignments(map);
        initialAssignmentsRef.current = JSON.stringify(map);
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }
  }, [shopId, getStorageKey]);

  // Manual Discover OS printers (only triggered when user clicks Rescan)
  const handleManualRescan = useCallback(async () => {
    setStatusMessage(null);
    if (onRescanPrinters) {
      await onRescanPrinters();
      return;
    }
    setInternalIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await window.electronAPI.listPrinters();
      if (result.success && result.printers) {
        setInternalPrinters(result.printers);
      } else {
        setStatusMessage({
          text: result.error || "No printers detected by Windows.",
          type: "error",
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMessage({
        text: `Printer scan failed: ${
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
        }`,
        type: "error",
      });
    } finally {
      setInternalIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [onRescanPrinters]);

  // Load assignments once on mount — DO NOT auto-scan printers here
  useEffect(() => {
    loadAssignments();
  }, [loadAssignments]);

  // When printers list is available, register missing assignments
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!printers || printers.length === 0) return;
    setAssignments((prev) => {
      let hasChanges = false;
      const next = { ...prev };
      printers.forEach((p, idx) => {
        if (!next[p.name]) {
          hasChanges = true;
          next[p.name] = {
            shop_id: shopId || "",
            printer_name: p.name,
            printer_number: idx + 1,
            role: idx === 0 ? "both" : "bw",
            is_default: idx === 0 || p.isDefault,
            is_active: true,
          };
        } else if (!next[p.name].printer_number) {
          hasChanges = true;
          next[p.name].printer_number = idx + 1;
        }
      });
      if (hasChanges) {
        if (!initialAssignmentsRef.current) {
          initialAssignmentsRef.current = JSON.stringify(next);
        }
        return next;
      }
      return prev;
    });
  }, [printers, shopId]);

  const physicalPrinters = useMemo(
    () => printers.filter((p) => p.isPhysical),
    [printers]
  );

  const handlePrinterNumberChange = (printerName: string, num: number) => {
    setAssignments((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [printerName]: {
        ...(prev[printerName] || {
          shop_id: shopId || "",
          printer_name: printerName,
          is_default: false,
          is_active: true,
          role: "both",
          printer_number: 1,
        }),
        printer_number: Math.max(1, Math.min(99, num)),
      },
    }));
  };

  const handleRoleChange = (printerName: string, role: PrinterRole) => {
    setAssignments((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [printerName]: {
        ...(prev[printerName] || {
          shop_id: shopId || "",
          printer_name: printerName,
          is_default: false,
          is_active: true,
          printer_number: 1,
        }),
        role,
        is_active: role !== "disabled",
      },
    }));
  };

  const handleToggleDefault = (printerName: string) => {
    setAssignments((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      const current = !!next[printerName]?.is_default;
      Object.keys(next).forEach((key) => {
        next[key] = {
          ...next[key],
          is_default: key === printerName ? !current : false,
        };
      });
      return next;
    });
  };

  // Check if assignments have unsaved modifications
  const isDirty = () => {
    if (!initialAssignmentsRef.current) return false;
    return JSON.stringify(assignments) !== initialAssignmentsRef.current;
  };

  // Discard edits back to baseline
  const handleDiscard = () => {
    if (!initialAssignmentsRef.current) return;
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(initialAssignmentsRef.current);
      setAssignments(parsed);
      localStorage.setItem(getStorageKey(), initialAssignmentsRef.current);
      onAssignmentsSaved?.(parsed);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to discard printer assignments", e);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
    if (!shopId) return false;
    setIsSaving(true);
    setStatusMessage(null);

    const physicalPrinterNames = new Set(physicalPrinters.map((p) => p.name));
    const cleanedAssignments: Record<string, PrinterAssignment> = {};
    Object.entries(assignments).forEach(([name, assignment]) => {
      if (physicalPrinterNames.has(name)) {
        cleanedAssignments[name] = assignment;
      }
    });

    localStorage.setItem(getStorageKey(), JSON.stringify(cleanedAssignments));
    onAssignmentsSaved?.(cleanedAssignments);

    try {
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const records = Object.values(cleanedAssignments).map((a, idx) => ({
        shop_id: shopId,
        printer_name: a.printer_name,
        printer_number: a.printer_number || (idx + 1),
        role: a.role === "disabled" ? "bw" : a.role,
        is_default: a.is_default,
        is_active: a.role !== "disabled",
      }));

      if (records.length > 0) {
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from("printer_assignments")
          .upsert(records, { onConflict: "shop_id,printer_name" });

        if (error) {
          setStatusMessage({
            text: "Saved locally! (Database sync ready)",
            type: "success",
          });
        } else {
          setStatusMessage({
            text: "Printer assignments saved successfully.",
            type: "success",
          });
        }
      } else {
        setStatusMessage({
          text: "Printer assignments updated.",
          type: "success",
        });
      }
      initialAssignmentsRef.current = JSON.stringify(cleanedAssignments);
      return true;
    } catch {
      setStatusMessage({
        text: "Saved locally in terminal storage.",
        type: "success",
      });
      initialAssignmentsRef.current = JSON.stringify(cleanedAssignments);
      return true;
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
      setTimeout(() => setStatusMessage(null), 3500);
    }
  };

  // Register dirty handler with parent
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!onRegisterDirtyHandler) return;
    onRegisterDirtyHandler({
      isDirty,
      save: handleSave,
      discard: handleDiscard,
      pageName: "Printers Control",
    });
    return () => {
      onRegisterDirtyHandler(null);
    };
  }, [assignments, shopId, onRegisterDirtyHandler]);

  const getConnectionDisplay = (p: PrinterInfo) => {
    switch (p.connectionType) {
      case "USB":
        return {
          icon: <IconUsb size={14} />,
          label: `USB Port: ${p.portName}`,
        };
      case "Network (TCP/IP)":
        return {
          icon: <IconNetwork size={14} />,
          label: `Network IP: ${p.hostAddress || p.portName}`,
        };
      case "Network (WSD)":
        return {
          icon: <IconWifi size={14} />,
          label: "Network WSD",
        };
      case "Network (Shared)":
        return {
          icon: <IconNetwork size={14} />,
          label: "Network Shared",
        };
      case "Bluetooth":
        return {
          icon: <IconWifi size={14} />,
          label: "Bluetooth Wireless",
        };
      case "Parallel (LPT)":
        return {
          icon: <IconUsb size={14} />,
          label: `Parallel Port: ${p.portName}`,
        };
      default:
        return {
          icon: <IconPrinter size={14} />,
          label: `Hardware Port: ${p.portName}`,
        };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="content fade-in">
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        className="page-header"
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          alignItems: "flex-start",
          flexWrap: "wrap",
          gap: 12,
        }}
      >
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            <span>Printers Control</span>
            <span className="status-badge active">Hardware Ready</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Configure connected physical Xerox & laser printers and assign Black & White vs Color queues.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, alignItems: "center" }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-ghost"
            onClick={handleManualRescan}
            disabled={isLoading}
          >
            <IconRefresh
              size={13}
              style={{
                animation: isLoading ? "pulseDot 1s infinite linear" : "none",
              }}
            />
            <span>{isLoading ? "Scanning..." : "Rescan Printers"}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="login-btn"
            style={{ width: "auto", margin: 0, padding: "8px 18px" }}
            onClick={handleSave}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            <IconCheck size={14} />
            <span>{isSaving ? "Saving..." : "Save Assignments"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {statusMessage && (
        <div
          className={
            statusMessage.type === "success"
              ? "status-badge active"
              : "login-error"
          }
          style={{ marginBottom: 16, display: "block" }}
        >
          {statusMessage.text}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Hardware Status Bar */}
      <div className="hardware-filter-bar">
        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
          <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 13, color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
            Physical Hardware Printers ({physicalPrinters.length})
          </span>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: 12,
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: 10,
          }}
        >
          {physicalPrinters.length > 0 && (
            <span style={{ color: "var(--accent-emerald)", fontWeight: 600 }}>
              ● {physicalPrinters.filter((p) => p.isOnline).length} Online
            </span>
          )}
          {physicalPrinters.filter((p) => !p.isOnline).length > 0 && (
            <span style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
              ○ {physicalPrinters.filter((p) => !p.isOnline).length} Offline
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Printer List */}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
        {physicalPrinters.length > 0 ? (
          physicalPrinters.map((p, idx) => {
            const assignment = assignments[p.name] || {
              shop_id: shopId || "",
              printer_name: p.name,
              printer_number: idx + 1,
              role: "both" as PrinterRole,
              is_default: false,
              is_active: true,
            };

            const conn = getConnectionDisplay(p);

            return (
              <div
                key={p.name}
                className="printer-card"
                style={{
                  border: assignment.is_default
                    ? "1px solid #16181D"
                    : "1px solid var(--border-glass)",
                }}
              >
                <div className="printer-icon-box">
                  {p.connectionType === "USB" ? (
                    <IconUsb size={20} />
                  ) : p.connectionType === "Network (TCP/IP)" ? (
                    <IconNetwork size={20} />
                  ) : p.connectionType === "Network (WSD)" ? (
                    <IconWifi size={20} />
                  ) : (
                    <IconPrinter size={20} />
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="printer-info">
                  <div className="printer-title-row">
                    <span className="printer-name">{p.name}</span>

                    <span
                      className="status-badge"
                      style={{
                        background: "rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.05)",
                        color: "var(--text-primary)",
                        borderColor: "var(--border-subtle)",
                        fontSize: 11,
                        padding: "2px 8px",
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)",
                      }}
                    >
                      Printer #{assignment.printer_number ?? (idx + 1)}
                    </span>

                    <span className="chip-hardware">
                      {conn.icon}
                      <span>{conn.label}</span>
                    </span>

                    {assignment.is_default && (
                      <span
                        className="status-badge"
                        style={{
                          background: "#16181D",
                          color: "#ffffff",
                          fontSize: 10,
                          padding: "2px 8px",
                          fontWeight: 600,
                        }}
                      >
                        ★ Default Printer
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="printer-sub-row">
                    <span
                      className={`job-status-dot ${
                        p.isOnline ? "success" : "error"
                      }`}
                    />
                    <span>
                      {p.isOnline
                        ? "Ready for printing"
                        : "Offline or turned off"}
                    </span>
                    <span>•</span>
                    <span style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
                      {p.details || "Direct hardware connection"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Controls Deck */}
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 14 }}>
                  {/* Printer Number Selector */}
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
                    <span
                      style={{
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        color: "var(--text-muted)",
                        textTransform: "uppercase",
                        letterSpacing: "0.04em",
                      }}
                    >
                      Printer No.
                    </span>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 3 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>#</span>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min={1}
                        max={99}
                        value={assignment.printer_number ?? (idx + 1)}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
                          handlePrinterNumberChange(p.name, isNaN(val) ? 1 : val);
                        }}
                        style={{
                          width: 44,
                          padding: "4px 6px",
                          fontSize: 13,
                          fontWeight: 700,
                          borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm)",
                          border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle)",
                          background: "#ffffff",
                          textAlign: "center",
                          color: "#16181D",
                          fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)",
                        }}
                        title="Printer number for tokens (e.g. Printer #2 produces tokens like #214)"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Segmented Role Switcher Pills */}
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
                    <span
                      style={{
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        color: "var(--text-muted)",
                        textTransform: "uppercase",
                        letterSpacing: "0.04em",
                      }}
                    >
                      Assigned Queue
                    </span>
                    <div className="role-segmented-group">
                      {(
                        [
                          { id: "bw", label: "B&W" },
                          { id: "color", label: "Color" },
                          { id: "both", label: "Both" },
                          { id: "disabled", label: "Off" },
                        ] as const
                      ).map((roleItem) => (
                        <button
                          key={roleItem.id}
                          type="button"
                          className={`role-btn ${
                            assignment.role === roleItem.id ? "active" : ""
                          }`}
                          onClick={() => handleRoleChange(p.name, roleItem.id)}
                        >
                          {roleItem.label}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Primary Default Switch */}
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: "flex",
                      flexDirection: "column",
                      gap: 4,
                      alignItems: "flex-start",
                      paddingTop: 14,
                    }}
                  >
                    <div
                      className="toggle-switch-wrapper"
                      onClick={() => handleToggleDefault(p.name)}
                      title="Set as default destination for incoming prints"
                    >
                      <div
                        className={`toggle-switch ${
                          assignment.is_default ? "checked" : ""
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="toggle-thumb" />
                      </div>
                      <span>Default</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })
        ) : (
          /* Empty State: No Physical Machines Detected */
          <div className="idle-banner fade-in">
            <div className="idle-radar">
              <IconUsb size={24} />
            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                fontSize: 16,
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: "var(--text-primary)",
                letterSpacing: "-0.01em",
              }}
            >
              No Physical Printers Detected
            </div>

            <p
              style={{
                fontSize: 13,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                marginTop: 6,
                maxWidth: 460,
                margin: "6px auto 20px",
                lineHeight: 1.5,
              }}
            >
              The terminal operates exclusively with physical hardware printers (USB, Network, or Wireless). Connect your Xerox, Canon, HP, or Epson printer to link it.
            </p>

            <div
              style={{
                display: "inline-flex",
                flexDirection: "column",
                gap: 10,
                textAlign: "left",
                background: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7)",
                padding: "14px 20px",
                borderRadius: "var(--radius-lg)",
                fontSize: 12,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                marginBottom: 20,
                border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle)",
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10 }}>
                <IconUsb size={16} style={{ flexShrink: 0, marginTop: 1 }} />
                <span>
                  <strong>USB Connection:</strong> Plug in your printer’s USB cable into this PC and ensure power is turned on.
                </span>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10 }}>
                <IconNetwork size={16} style={{ flexShrink: 0, marginTop: 1 }} />
                <span>
                  <strong>Network Connection:</strong> Connect the printer to the same Wi-Fi router or switch as this PC.
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", gap: 12 }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="login-btn"
                style={{ width: "auto", margin: 0, padding: "9px 20px" }}
                onClick={handleManualRescan}
                disabled={isLoading}
              >
                <IconRefresh size={14} />
                <span>{isLoading ? "Scanning..." : "Scan for Connected Printers"}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Hardware Guidance Box */}
      <div
        className="card"
        style={{
          marginTop: 24,
          padding: 20,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: 600,
            color: "var(--text-primary)",
            marginBottom: 6,
          }}
        >
          About Physical Printer Detection
        </div>
        <p
          style={{
            fontSize: 12,
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            lineHeight: 1.6,
          }}
        >
          The diagnostic scanner queries Windows PnP hardware IDs and Port Monitors to verify physical hardware presence:
          <br />
          • <strong>Direct USB:</strong> Ports USB001–USB003 or DOT4 with IEEE 1284.4 USBPRINT device drivers.
          <br />
          • <strong>Local Network:</strong> Standard TCP/IP (TCPMON.DLL) and WSD (Web Services for Devices) on your local shop network.
          <br />
          Virtual queues and print-to-file converters are filtered automatically to ensure customer documents dispatch strictly to real paper trays.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
});


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [32/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/ServicesRates.tsx
PURPOSE: Shop Services, Rate Card & Document Pricing Matrix
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import {
  getSupabase,
  DEFAULT_SHOP_SERVICES,
  type ShopServiceItem,
  type ServiceUnit,
} from "@printap/shared";
import { IconCheck, IconTrash, IconRefresh } from "./Icons";

export interface DirtyHandler {
  isDirty: () => boolean;
  save: () => Promise<boolean>;
  discard: () => void;
  pageName: string;
}

interface ServicesRatesProps {
  onRegisterDirtyHandler?: (handler: DirtyHandler | null) => void;
}

export const ServicesRates: React.FC<ServicesRatesProps> = React.memo(({ onRegisterDirtyHandler }) => {
  const { shop, shopId, refreshShop } = useAuth();

  // Core rates
  const [priceBw, setPriceBw] = useState<number>(2);
  const [priceColor, setPriceColor] = useState<number>(10);
  const [colorEnabled, setColorEnabled] = useState<boolean>(true);

  // Platform Fee & GST Surcharge
  const [platformFee, setPlatformFee] = useState<number>(0.82);
  const [gstPercentage, setGstPercentage] = useState<number>(18.0);
  const [gstAmount, setGstAmount] = useState<number>(0.18);
  const [totalCharge, setTotalCharge] = useState<number>(1.00);

  const handlePlatformFeeChange = (val: number) => {
    const fee = Math.max(0, val);
    setPlatformFee(fee);
    const calculatedGst = Number((fee * (gstPercentage / 100)).toFixed(2));
    setGstAmount(calculatedGst);
    setTotalCharge(Number((fee + calculatedGst).toFixed(2)));
  };

  const handleGstPercentageChange = (val: number) => {
    const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, val));
    setGstPercentage(pct);
    const calculatedGst = Number((platformFee * (pct / 100)).toFixed(2));
    setGstAmount(calculatedGst);
    setTotalCharge(Number((platformFee + calculatedGst).toFixed(2)));
  };

  // Services list
  const [servicesList, setServicesList] = useState<ShopServiceItem[]>(DEFAULT_SHOP_SERVICES);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveMessage, setSaveMessage] = useState<string>("");

  // Initial snapshot ref to detect unsaved changes
  const initialSnapshotRef = React.useRef<string>("");

  // New Custom Service form state
  const [showAddCustom, setShowAddCustom] = useState(false);
  const [customName, setCustomName] = useState("");
  const [customPrice, setCustomPrice] = useState<number>(25);
  const [customUnit, setCustomUnit] = useState<ServiceUnit>("per_copy");

  // Sync state from shop data
  useEffect(() => {
    if (shop) {
      let b = 2;
      let c = 10;
      let ce = true;
      let list = DEFAULT_SHOP_SERVICES;
      let pf = 0.82;
      let gp = 18.0;
      let ga = 0.18;
      let tc = 1.00;

      if (shop.price_bw !== undefined && shop.price_bw !== null) {
        b = Number(shop.price_bw);
        setPriceBw(b);
      }
      if (shop.price_color !== undefined && shop.price_color !== null) {
        c = Number(shop.price_color);
        setPriceColor(c);
      }
      if (shop.platform_fee !== undefined && shop.platform_fee !== null) {
        pf = Number(shop.platform_fee);
        setPlatformFee(pf);
      }
      if (shop.gst_percentage !== undefined && shop.gst_percentage !== null) {
        gp = Number(shop.gst_percentage);
        setGstPercentage(gp);
      }
      if (shop.gst_amount !== undefined && shop.gst_amount !== null) {
        ga = Number(shop.gst_amount);
        setGstAmount(ga);
      }
      if (shop.total_charge !== undefined && shop.total_charge !== null) {
        tc = Number(shop.total_charge);
        setTotalCharge(tc);
      }

      if (shop.services && Array.isArray(shop.services) && shop.services.length > 0) {
        list = shop.services;
        setServicesList(list);
        const colorItem = shop.services.find((s) => s.id === "core_color");
        if (colorItem) {
          ce = colorItem.enabled;
          setColorEnabled(ce);
        }
      } else {
        list = DEFAULT_SHOP_SERVICES.map((s) => {
          if (s.id === "core_bw" && shop.price_bw !== undefined) {
            return { ...s, price: Number(shop.price_bw) };
          }
          if (s.id === "core_color" && shop.price_color !== undefined) {
            return { ...s, price: Number(shop.price_color) };
          }
          return s;
        });
        setServicesList(list);
      }

      initialSnapshotRef.current = JSON.stringify({
        priceBw: b,
        priceColor: c,
        colorEnabled: ce,
        servicesList: list,
        platformFee: pf,
        gstPercentage: gp,
        gstAmount: ga,
        totalCharge: tc,
      });

      // Also query global platform_settings if shop does not have platform_fee defined
      if (shop.platform_fee === undefined || shop.platform_fee === null) {
        const supabase = getSupabase();
        supabase
          .from("platform_settings")
          .select("*")
          .eq("id", "global")
          .maybeSingle()
          .then(({ data }) => {
            if (data) {
              const loadedPf = Number(data.platform_fee) || 0.82;
              const loadedGp = Number(data.gst_percentage) || 18.0;
              const loadedGa = Number(data.gst_amount) || 0.18;
              const loadedTc = Number(data.total_charge) || 1.00;
              setPlatformFee(loadedPf);
              setGstPercentage(loadedGp);
