    <path
      fill="#EA4335"
      d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.06l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.52 6.16-4.52z"
    />
  </svg>
);

export const Login: React.FC = () => {
  const {
    login,
    loginWithGoogle,
    isGoogleSigningIn,
    cancelGoogleLogin,
    reopenGoogleBrowser,
    sendResetOtp,
    resetPassword,
  } = useAuth();

  // Login form state
  const [emailInput, setEmailInput] = useState("");
  const [passwordInput, setPasswordInput] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [rememberEmail, setRememberEmail] = useState(true);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Forgot Password modal state
  const [showForgotModal, setShowForgotModal] = useState(false);
  const [forgotStep, setForgotStep] = useState<1 | 2>(1);
  const [forgotEmail, setForgotEmail] = useState("");
  const [forgotOtp, setForgotOtp] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmNewPassword, setConfirmNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [forgotLoading, setForgotLoading] = useState(false);
  const [forgotError, setForgotError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [forgotSuccess, setForgotSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [resendCooldown, setResendCooldown] = useState(0);

  // Load saved email on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY);
    if (saved) {
      setEmailInput(saved);
      setForgotEmail(saved);
    }
  }, []);

  // Cooldown countdown timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (resendCooldown <= 0) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setResendCooldown((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : 0));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [resendCooldown]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const cleanId = emailInput.trim();
    if (!cleanId) {
      setErrorMessage("Please enter your email address or shop handle.");
      return;
    }
    if (!passwordInput) {
      setErrorMessage("Please enter your password.");
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setErrorMessage(null);

    const result = await login(cleanId, passwordInput);
    if (!result.success) {
      setErrorMessage(result.error || "Terminal authentication failed.");
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    } else {
      if (rememberEmail) {
        localStorage.setItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY, cleanId);
      } else {
        localStorage.removeItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleGoogleSignIn = async () => {
    setErrorMessage(null);
    const result = await loginWithGoogle();
    if (!result.success && result.error && result.error !== "Login cancelled by user.") {
      setErrorMessage(result.error);
    }
  };

  // Step 1: Send OTP for password reset
  const handleSendResetCode = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const emailCheck = validateEmail(forgotEmail);
    if (!emailCheck.valid) {
      setForgotError(emailCheck.reason || "Please enter a valid registered email address.");
      return;
    }

    setForgotLoading(true);
    setForgotError(null);

    const res = await sendResetOtp(emailCheck.normalizedEmail);
    if (res.success) {
      setForgotStep(2);
      setResendCooldown(60);
    } else {
      setForgotError(res.error || "Failed to send reset code.");
    }
    setForgotLoading(false);
  };

  // Step 2: Confirm OTP & set new password
  const handleResetPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (forgotOtp.trim().length !== 6) {
      setForgotError("Please enter the 6-digit verification code.");
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword.length < 8) {
      setForgotError("Password must be at least 8 characters long.");
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword !== confirmNewPassword) {
      setForgotError("Passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    setForgotLoading(true);
    setForgotError(null);

    const res = await resetPassword(forgotEmail.trim(), forgotOtp.trim(), newPassword);
    if (res.success) {
      setForgotSuccess("Password reset successfully! You can now log in.");
      setEmailInput(forgotEmail.trim());
      setPasswordInput(newPassword);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setShowForgotModal(false);
        setForgotStep(1);
        setForgotOtp("");
        setNewPassword("");
        setConfirmNewPassword("");
        setForgotSuccess(null);
      }, 1500);
    } else {
      setForgotError(res.error || "Password reset failed.");
    }
    setForgotLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="login-container">
      <div className="login-card fade-in">
        <h1 className="login-title">Counter Terminal</h1>
        <p className="login-subtitle">
          Sign in with your registered shop account to connect your counter terminal.
        </p>

        {/* Google Sign-In Option */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 20 }}>
          {isGoogleSigningIn ? (
            <div className="login-waiting-card fade-in">
              <div className="login-waiting-spinner" />
              <div style={{ textAlign: "left", flex: 1 }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                  Authorizing with Google...
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", marginTop: 2 }}>
                  Check your browser tab to complete sign-in.
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6 }}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={reopenGoogleBrowser}
                  className="btn-ghost"
                  style={{ fontSize: 11, padding: "4px 8px" }}
                  title="Reopen login tab in browser"
                >
                  Reopen
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={cancelGoogleLogin}
                  className="btn-ghost"
                  style={{ fontSize: 11, padding: "4px 8px", color: "var(--accent-rose, #f43f5e)" }}
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleGoogleSignIn}
              disabled={isSubmitting || isGoogleSigningIn}
              className="login-google-btn"
            >
              <GoogleIcon size={18} />
              <span>Continue with Google</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Divider */}
        <div className="login-divider">
          <span>or sign in with password</span>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
          {/* Email Input */}
          <div style={{ textAlign: "left", marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <label
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                display: "block",
              }}
            >
              Shop Email or Subdomain Handle
            </label>
          </div>
          <input
            type="text"
            className="login-input"
            placeholder="e.g. rahul@gmail.com or apex-prints"
            value={emailInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setEmailInput(e.target.value);
              if (errorMessage) setErrorMessage(null);
            }}
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            autoFocus
            required
          />

          {/* Password Input */}
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              justifyContent: "space-between",
              alignItems: "center",
              marginTop: 14,
              marginBottom: 6,
            }}
          >
            <label
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              }}
            >
              Terminal Password
            </label>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
              style={{
                background: "none",
                border: "none",
                fontSize: 11,
                color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                cursor: "pointer",
                padding: 0,
              }}
            >
              {showPassword ? "Hide" : "Show"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <input
            type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
            className="login-input"
            placeholder="Enter your password"
            value={passwordInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setPasswordInput(e.target.value);
              if (errorMessage) setErrorMessage(null);
            }}
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            required
          />

          {/* Remember Email & Forgot Password */}
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              justifyContent: "space-between",
              alignItems: "center",
              marginTop: 12,
              fontSize: 12,
            }}
          >
            <label
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                gap: 6,
                cursor: "pointer",
                color: "var(--text-muted)",
              }}
            >
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={rememberEmail}
                onChange={(e) => setRememberEmail(e.target.checked)}
                style={{ cursor: "pointer", accentColor: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)" }}
              />
              Remember Email
            </label>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowForgotModal(true);
                setForgotEmail(emailInput || "");
                setForgotError(null);
              }}
              style={{
                background: "none",
                border: "none",
                color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                cursor: "pointer",
                padding: 0,
              }}
            >
              Forgot Password?
            </button>
          </div>

          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="login-error" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
              <div>{errorMessage}</div>
              {errorMessage.toLowerCase().includes("onboard") && (
                <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const onboardUrl = "https://www.mellod.in/onboard";
                      if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
                        window.electronAPI.openExternal(onboardUrl);
                      } else {
                        window.open(onboardUrl, "_blank");
                      }
                    }}
                    style={{
                      background: "none",
                      border: "none",
                      color: "#fff",
                      textDecoration: "underline",
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      cursor: "pointer",
                      padding: 0,
                    }}
                  >
                    Open Onboarding in Browser →
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Connect Button */}
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="login-btn"
            style={{ marginTop: 20 }}
            disabled={isSubmitting || !emailInput.trim() || !passwordInput}
          >
            {isSubmitting ? "Connecting Terminal..." : "Connect Counter Terminal"}
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* Onboarding Link */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 16, fontSize: 12, color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
          Need to onboard a new shop?{" "}
          <a
            href="https://www.mellod.in/onboard"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            style={{
              color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
              fontWeight: 600,
              textDecoration: "none",
            }}
          >
            Register on Web →
          </a>
        </div>

        {/* Trust Badges */}
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            gap: 8,
            marginTop: 24,
            fontSize: 12,
            color: "var(--text-muted)",
          }}
        >
          <IconShield size={14} color="var(--accent-emerald)" />
          <span>Zero Cloud Storage • Direct Peer-to-Peer</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MODAL: FORGOT & RESET PASSWORD */}
      {showForgotModal && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: "fixed",
            inset: 0,
            background: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75)",
            backdropFilter: "blur(4px)",
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            zIndex: 1000,
            padding: 16,
          }}
        >
          <div
            className="login-card fade-in"
            style={{
              maxWidth: 400,
              width: "100%",
              margin: 0,
              background: "var(--surface-primary, #1e1e24)",
              border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #333)",
            }}
          >
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, margin: "0 0 6px 0", color: "#fff" }}>
              Reset Terminal Password
            </h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--text-muted)", margin: "0 0 16px 0" }}>
              {forgotStep === 1
                ? "Enter your registered shop owner email to receive a 6-digit reset code."
                : `Enter the 6-digit code sent to ${forgotEmail} and choose a new password.`}
            </p>

            {forgotError && (
              <div className="login-error" style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
                {forgotError}
              </div>
            )}
            {forgotSuccess && (
              <div
                style={{
                  background: "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)",
                  border: "1px solid #10b981",
                  color: "#34d399",
                  padding: 10,
                  borderRadius: 8,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  marginBottom: 14,
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  gap: 6,
                }}
              >
                <IconCheck size={14} />
                <span>{forgotSuccess}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {forgotStep === 1 ? (
              <form onSubmit={handleSendResetCode}>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  className="login-input"
                  placeholder="Enter registered email"
                  value={forgotEmail}
                  onChange={(e) => setForgotEmail(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={forgotLoading}
                  autoFocus
                  required
                />
                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 16 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-ghost"
                    style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}
                    onClick={() => setShowForgotModal(false)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="login-btn"
                    style={{ flex: 1, marginTop: 0 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading || !forgotEmail.trim()}
                  >
                    {forgotLoading ? "Sending Code..." : "Send Reset Code"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleResetPassword}>
                <div style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    6-Digit Verification Code
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    maxLength={6}
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="e.g. 123456"
                    value={forgotOtp}
                    onChange={(e) => setForgotOtp(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""))}
                    style={{ letterSpacing: 4, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 18 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    autoFocus
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    New Password (min. 8 characters)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="New password"
                    value={newPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    Confirm New Password
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="Confirm new password"
                    value={confirmNewPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setConfirmNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 14, fontSize: 11 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setForgotStep(1)}
                    style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: "var(--text-muted)", cursor: "pointer", padding: 0 }}
                  >
                    ← Change Email
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={async () => {
                      if (resendCooldown > 0) return;
                      await sendResetOtp(forgotEmail.trim());
                      setResendCooldown(60);
                    }}
                    disabled={resendCooldown > 0 || forgotLoading}
                    style={{
                      background: "none",
                      border: "none",
                      color: resendCooldown > 0 ? "var(--text-muted)" : "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                      cursor: resendCooldown > 0 ? "default" : "pointer",
                      padding: 0,
                      fontWeight: 600,
                    }}
                  >
                    {resendCooldown > 0 ? `Resend code in ${resendCooldown}s` : "Resend Code"}
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-ghost"
                    style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}
                    onClick={() => setShowForgotModal(false)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="login-btn"
                    style={{ flex: 1, marginTop: 0 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading || forgotOtp.length !== 6 || newPassword.length < 8}
                  >
                    {forgotLoading ? "Resetting..." : "Set New Password"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE [38/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/UpdateScreen.tsx
PURPOSE: Software Auto-Updater Modal (Download Progress & Restart)
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React from "react";

interface UpdateScreenProps {
  stage: "checking" | "available" | "downloading" | "downloaded" | "error" | "not-available";
  progress?: number;
  error?: string;
  onRestart: () => void;
  onDismiss: () => void;
}

export function UpdateScreen({ stage, progress, error, onRestart, onDismiss }: UpdateScreenProps) {
  // Silent checks should not block the app
  if (stage === "checking" || stage === "not-available") {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: "fixed",
        inset: 0,
        backgroundColor: "rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.72)",
        backdropFilter: "blur(8px)",
        WebkitBackdropFilter: "blur(8px)",
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
        zIndex: 10000,
        padding: 24,
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="fade-in"
        style={{
          backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
          border: "1px solid var(--border-active, #16181D)",
          borderRadius: "var(--radius-lg, 2px)",
          padding: "36px 32px",
          maxWidth: 440,
          width: "100%",
          textAlign: "center",
          boxShadow: "var(--shadow-glass-modal, 0 20px 48px rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.16))",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: 48,
            height: 48,
            backgroundColor: "var(--color-structure, #16181D)",
            color: "#FFFFFF",
            borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 1px)",
            margin: "0 auto 20px",
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
          }}
        >
          <svg style={{ width: 24, height: 24 }} fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
            <path
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
              strokeWidth={2}
              d="M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-8l-4-4m0 0L8 8m4-4v12"
            />
          </svg>
        </div>

        <h2
          style={{
            fontSize: 20,
            fontWeight: 700,
            fontFamily: "var(--font-sans)",
            color: "var(--text-primary, #16181D)",
            marginBottom: 8,
            letterSpacing: "-0.02em",
          }}
        >
          {stage === "available" && "Software Update Available"}
          {stage === "downloading" && "Downloading Package..."}
          {stage === "downloaded" && "Update Ready to Install"}
          {stage === "error" && "Update Verification"}
        </h2>

        <p
          style={{
            fontSize: 13,
            color: "var(--text-secondary, #424750)",
            lineHeight: 1.5,
            marginBottom: 24,
            fontFamily: "var(--font-sans)",
          }}
        >
          {stage === "available" && "A new version of the counter terminal is available and preparing to download."}
          {stage === "downloading" && "Downloading release assets in the background. Please keep the app open."}
          {stage === "downloaded" && "The update has been securely verified and downloaded. Restart now to apply."}
          {stage === "error" && (error || "Unable to retrieve update package.")}
        </p>

        {stage === "downloading" && (
          <div
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              height: 6,
              backgroundColor: "rgba(209, 200, 204, 0.5)",
              borderRadius: 1,
              overflow: "hidden",
              marginBottom: 16,
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                height: "100%",
                width: `${Math.round(progress || 0)}%`,
                backgroundColor: "var(--color-structure, #16181D)",
                transition: "width 0.3s ease",
              }}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {stage === "downloaded" && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onRestart}
            className="btn-primary"
            style={{ width: "100%", padding: "11px 18px", fontSize: 13 }}
          >
            Restart & Install Update
          </button>
        )}

        {stage === "error" && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onDismiss}
            className="btn-secondary"
            style={{ width: "100%", padding: "10px 18px", fontSize: 13 }}
          >
            Continue to Counter Terminal
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE [39/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/UnsavedChangesModal.tsx
PURPOSE: Modal Guard for Unsaved Changes Across Forms
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React from "react";

interface UnsavedChangesModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  pageName: string;
  isSaving: boolean;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onDiscard: () => void;
  onSave: () => void;
}

export const UnsavedChangesModal: React.FC<UnsavedChangesModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  pageName,
  isSaving,
  onCancel,
  onDiscard,
  onSave,
}) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={onCancel}>
      <div
        className="confirm-dialog-card"
        style={{ maxWidth: 440 }}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="confirm-dialog-title">Unsaved Changes</div>
        <div
          className="confirm-dialog-body"
          style={{ color: "var(--text-secondary)", lineHeight: 1.5, margin: "12px 0 20px" }}
        >
          You have unsaved changes in <strong>{pageName}</strong>. Would you like to save your
          changes before leaving this section?
        </div>
        <div
          className="confirm-dialog-actions"
          style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-cancel"
            onClick={onCancel}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-discard"
            onClick={onDiscard}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            Discard
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-save"
            onClick={onSave}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            {isSaving ? "Saving..." : "Save & Continue"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE [40/43]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
PURPOSE: Global React Exception Boundary
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { Component, ErrorInfo, ReactNode } from "react";

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
  fallbackTitle?: string;
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
  errorInfo: ErrorInfo | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  public state: State = {
    hasError: false,
    error: null,
    errorInfo: null,
  };

  public static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true, error, errorInfo: null };
  }

  public componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error("[ErrorBoundary caught an uncaught exception]:", error, errorInfo);
    this.setState({ error, errorInfo });
  }

  private handleReload = () => {
    window.location.reload();
  };

  private handleReset = () => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null });
  };

  public render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div
          style={{
            minHeight: "100vh",
            display: "flex",
            flexDirection: "column",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            padding: 32,
            backgroundColor: "var(--bg-canvas, #F0EBED)",
            color: "var(--text-primary, #16181D)",
            fontFamily: "var(--font-sans, system-ui, sans-serif)",
            textAlign: "center",
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              maxWidth: 520,
              width: "100%",
              backgroundColor: "var(--bg-card, #FFFFFF)",
              border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #D1C8CC)",
              borderRadius: "var(--radius-lg, 2px)",
              padding: "36px 32px",
              boxShadow: "0 10px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)",
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                display: "inline-flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "center",
                width: 48,
                height: 48,
                borderRadius: "50%",
                backgroundColor: "#FEE2E2",
                color: "#DC2626",
                marginBottom: 20,
                fontSize: 22,
                fontWeight: "bold",
              }}
            >
              !
            </div>

            <h1
              style={{
                fontSize: 20,
                fontWeight: 700,
                marginBottom: 10,
                letterSpacing: "-0.02em",
              }}
            >
              {this.props.fallbackTitle || "Terminal Rendering Exception"}
            </h1>

            <p
              style={{
                fontSize: 13,
                color: "var(--text-secondary, #424750)",
                lineHeight: 1.5,
                marginBottom: 20,
              }}
            >
              An unexpected error occurred while rendering the interface. Your print jobs and hardware
              connections remain safe in background memory.
            </p>

            {this.state.error && (
              <pre
                style={{
                  textAlign: "left",
                  fontSize: 11,
                  fontFamily: "var(--font-mono, monospace)",
                  backgroundColor: "#F9FAFB",
                  border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #D1C8CC)",
                  padding: 12,
                  borderRadius: 2,
                  color: "#991B1B",
                  overflowX: "auto",
                  maxHeight: 120,
                  marginBottom: 24,
                }}
              >
                {this.state.error.message}
              </pre>
            )}

            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, justifyContent: "center" }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={this.handleReset}
                className="btn-secondary"
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 18px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  cursor: "pointer",
                }}
              >
                Attempt Recovery
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={this.handleReload}
                className="btn-primary"
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 20px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  cursor: "pointer",
                }}
              >
                Reload Window
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PRINTAP SHOP AGENT — STRUCTURAL HIGH-CONTRAST DESIGN SYSTEM
   Base Background (#F0EBED) • Luminous Glow (#FFFFFF)
   Primary Text & Structure (#16181D) • Secondary Accent (#424750)
   Subtle Borders (#D1C8CC) • Sharp Wireframe Edges • Space Grotesk & JetBrains Mono
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root {
  /* ─── The Core Palette ─── */
  --bg-canvas: #F0EBED;                     /* Base Background: pale, washed-out pinkish-grey */
  --surface-glow: #FFFFFF;                  /* Surface / Glow: pure white luminous surface */
  --bg-card: #FFFFFF;                       /* Card backgrounds replicating luminous central glow */
  --bg-glass-card: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-card-hover: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-elevated: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-subtle: rgba(240, 235, 237, 0.65);
  --bg-glass-input: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-sidebar: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94);

  /* ─── Primary Text & Structure (#16181D) ─── */
  --text-primary: #16181D;                  /* Deep near-black slate for headings, primary body text */
  --color-structure: #16181D;               /* Thick borders, solid buttons, dark structural pillars */
  --border-active: #16181D;

  /* ─── Secondary Accent (#424750) ─── */
  --text-secondary: #424750;                /* Medium charcoal for secondary text, metadata */
  --accent-secondary: #424750;              /* Hover states, subtle UI icons */
  --text-muted: #5e6470;                    /* Technical metadata */
  --text-faint: #888e99;

  /* ─── Subtle Borders (#D1C8CC) ─── */
  --border-subtle: #D1C8CC;                 /* Dusty mauve-grey divider lines and 1px wireframe containers */
  --border-subtle-hover: #16181D;           /* High-contrast state */
  --border-glass: #D1C8CC;                  /* 1px crisp architectural wireframe borders */

  /* ─── High Contrast Architectural Accents (Avoiding saturated colors) ─── */
  --accent-emerald: #16181D;
  --accent-emerald-light: rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.05);

───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [43/43]: apps/web/src/lib/customer-signaling.ts
PURPOSE: Customer WebRTC Peer: SDP Offer, Chunked File Streaming, Status Listener
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
 * Customer-side WebRTC handler for Multi-File & Print Settings Streaming.
 *
 * Creates a peer connection, generates an SDP offer with ICE candidate preroll,
 * relays ICE candidates with early-candidate buffering, and transfers
 * the complete order manifest and file stream via RTCDataChannel.
 */

import {
  joinSignalingChannel,
  waitForChannelReady,
  broadcast,
  DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
  waitForIceGathering,
  type SignalPayload,
  type UploadedDocument,
  type QueueToken,
  type PaymentMethod,
  type PaymentStatus,
  type FinishingService,
  type TransferManifestMessage,
  type TransferFileHeaderMessage,
  type TransferFileEofMessage,
  type TransferOrderCompleteMessage,
} from "@printap/shared";

// 32KB safe WebRTC chunk size across iOS Safari, Android Chrome, and Windows/Mac
const CHUNK_SIZE = 32 * 1024;

export type TransferStatus =
  | "connecting"
  | "connected"
  | "sending"
  | "complete"
  | "error";

export interface TransferProgress {
  status: TransferStatus;
  /** 0–100 overall progress */
  percent: number;
  /** Bytes sent so far across all files */
  bytesSent: number;
  /** Total bytes of all files combined */
  totalBytes: number;
  /** 1-indexed number of the file currently being transferred */
  currentFileIndex?: number;
  /** Name of the current file being transferred */
  currentFileName?: string;
  /** Total number of files in this order */
  totalFiles?: number;
  error?: string;
}

export type ProgressCallback = (progress: TransferProgress) => void;

export interface SendOrderPayload {
  shopId: string;
  sessionId: string;
  documents: UploadedDocument[];
  token: QueueToken;
  paymentMethod: PaymentMethod;
  paymentStatus: PaymentStatus;
  totalAmount: number;
  finishingServices: FinishingService[];
}

/**
 * Connect to the shop's Electron agent and stream an entire order
 * (manifest + multiple files with print settings) over WebRTC.
 * Returns a Promise that resolves when all files have finished streaming,
 * or rejects if connection/transfer fails.
 */
export function sendOrderToShop(
  payload: SendOrderPayload,
  printTicket: string,
  onProgress: ProgressCallback
): Promise<void> {
  const { shopId, sessionId, documents } = payload;
  const totalBytes = documents.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.file.size, 0);

  return new Promise<void>(async (resolve, reject) => {
    let settled = false;
    let offerRetryTimer: any = null;
    let overallTimeoutTimer: any = null;
    let pc: RTCPeerConnection | null = null;
    let dc: RTCDataChannel | null = null;

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (offerRetryTimer) {
        clearInterval(offerRetryTimer);
        offerRetryTimer = null;
      }
      if (overallTimeoutTimer) {
        clearTimeout(overallTimeoutTimer);
        overallTimeoutTimer = null;
      }
    };

    const fail = (errorMessage: string) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      cleanup();

      console.error(`[customer] sendOrderToShop failed: ${errorMessage}`);
      onProgress({
        status: "error",
        percent: 0,
        bytesSent: 0,
        totalBytes,
        error: errorMessage,
      });

      try {
        dc?.close();
        pc?.close();
      } catch {}

      reject(new Error(errorMessage));
    };

    const succeed = () => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      cleanup();

      console.log(`[customer] sendOrderToShop successfully completed for session ${sessionId}`);
      onProgress({
        status: "complete",
        percent: 100,
        bytesSent: totalBytes,
        totalBytes,
        currentFileIndex: documents.length,
        currentFileName: documents[documents.length - 1]?.file.name,
        totalFiles: documents.length,
      });

      resolve();
    };

    // 1. Initial status
    onProgress({
      status: "connecting",
      percent: 0,
      bytesSent: 0,
      totalBytes,
      totalFiles: documents.length,
      currentFileIndex: 1,
      currentFileName: documents[0]?.file.name,
    });

    // 2. Timeout safeguard (35 seconds)
    overallTimeoutTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      fail(
        "Connection to counter terminal timed out. Please ensure the shop desktop app is running and connected."
      );
    }, 35_000);

    try {
      const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId);
      await waitForChannelReady(channel, 2000);

      // 4. Initialize RTCPeerConnection with unified resilient STUN servers & candidate pool
      pc = new RTCPeerConnection({
        iceServers: DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
        iceCandidatePoolSize: 10,
      });

      // 5. Create DataChannel before creating the offer
      dc = pc.createDataChannel("order-transfer", {
        ordered: true,
      });
      dc.binaryType = "arraybuffer";

      // 5. Early candidate buffer
      const earlyCandidates: RTCIceCandidateInit[] = [];
      let remoteDescriptionSet = false;

      // 6. Relay local ICE candidates
      pc.onicecandidate = (event) => {
        if (event.candidate && event.candidate.candidate) {
          broadcast(channel, "webrtc-ice", {
            sessionId,
            data: event.candidate.toJSON(),
          });
        }
      };

      // 7. Track connection state
      pc.onconnectionstatechange = () => {
        if (!pc) return;
        console.log(
          `[customer] Connection state: ${pc.connectionState} (ice: ${pc.iceConnectionState})`
        );
        if (pc.connectionState === "failed") {
          fail("WebRTC direct peer connection failed. NAT traversal could not find an open path.");
        }
      };

      pc.oniceconnectionstatechange = () => {
        if (!pc) return;
        console.log(`[customer] ICE state: ${pc.iceConnectionState}`);
        if (pc.iceConnectionState === "failed") {
          try {
            if (typeof pc.restartIce === "function") {
              console.log("[customer] Restarting ICE...");
              pc.restartIce();
            }
          } catch (e) {
            console.warn("[customer] restartIce error:", e);
          }
        }
      };

      // 8. Listen for shop answer
      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "webrtc-answer" }, async ({ payload: resPayload }) => {
        const { sessionId: answerSessionId, data } = resPayload as SignalPayload;
        if (answerSessionId !== sessionId || !pc) return;

        if (offerRetryTimer) {
          clearInterval(offerRetryTimer);
          offerRetryTimer = null;
        }

        console.log(`[customer] Received answer for session ${sessionId}`);
        try {
          if (pc.signalingState === "have-local-offer") {
            await pc.setRemoteDescription(
              new RTCSessionDescription(data as RTCSessionDescriptionInit)
            );
            remoteDescriptionSet = true;

            // Drain buffered early candidates
            if (earlyCandidates.length > 0) {
              console.log(
                `[customer] Draining ${earlyCandidates.length} buffered ICE candidates for ${sessionId}`
              );
              for (const cand of earlyCandidates) {
                try {
                  await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(cand));
                } catch (candErr) {
                  console.warn("[customer] Failed adding buffered candidate:", candErr);
                }
              }
              earlyCandidates.length = 0;
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn("[customer] setRemoteDescription error:", err);
        }
      });

      // 9. Listen for shop ICE candidates
      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "webrtc-ice" }, async ({ payload: resPayload }) => {
        const { sessionId: iceSessionId, data } = resPayload as SignalPayload;
        if (iceSessionId !== sessionId || !pc) return;

        const candidateInit = data as RTCIceCandidateInit;
        if (!candidateInit || !candidateInit.candidate) return;

        if (!remoteDescriptionSet || !pc.remoteDescription) {
          earlyCandidates.push(candidateInit);
          return;
        }

        try {
          await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(candidateInit));
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn(`[customer] Failed to add ICE candidate:`, err);
        }
      });

      // 10. Listen for terminal hardware errors
      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "order-status" }, ({ payload: resPayload }) => {
        const p = resPayload as SignalPayload;
        if (p.sessionId !== sessionId) return;
        if (p.status === "error") {
          fail(p.errorDetail || "Shop counter terminal reported a hardware/spooler error");
        }
      });

      // 11. DataChannel lifecycle
      dc.onopen = async () => {
        if (offerRetryTimer) {
          clearInterval(offerRetryTimer);
          offerRetryTimer = null;
        }

        console.log(`[customer] DataChannel opened — streaming order manifest & files`);
        onProgress({
          status: "connected",
          percent: 0,
          bytesSent: 0,
          totalBytes,
          totalFiles: documents.length,
          currentFileIndex: 1,
          currentFileName: documents[0]?.file.name,
        });

        try {
          await streamOrder(dc!, payload, printTicket, onProgress);
          succeed();
        } catch (err) {
          fail(`Transfer error: ${String(err)}`);
        }
      };

      dc.onerror = () => {
        fail("DataChannel connection interrupted during transfer");
      };

      // 12. Create and set local offer with ICE candidate gathering preroll
      const offer = await pc.createOffer();
      await pc.setLocalDescription(offer);

      // Wait up to 800ms so initial LAN & STUN candidates are embedded directly in offer SDP
      await waitForIceGathering(pc, 800);

      const sendOffer = async () => {
        if (!pc || pc.signalingState !== "have-local-offer") return;
        const localDesc = pc.localDescription || offer;
        console.log(`[customer] Transmitting offer for session ${sessionId}`);
        await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-offer", {
          sessionId,
          data: { type: localDesc.type, sdp: localDesc.sdp },
        });
      };

      await sendOffer();

      // Retry offer up to 4 times (every 2.5s) if connection has not established
      let offerRetries = 0;
      offerRetryTimer = setInterval(async () => {
        if (
          !pc ||
          pc.remoteDescription ||
          pc.signalingState === "stable" ||
          offerRetries >= 4
        ) {
          if (offerRetryTimer) {
            clearInterval(offerRetryTimer);
            offerRetryTimer = null;
          }
          return;
        }
        offerRetries++;
        console.log(
          `[customer] Re-transmitting offer (attempt ${offerRetries + 1}/5) for session ${sessionId}`
        );
        await sendOffer();
      }, 2500);
    } catch (err) {
      fail(String(err));
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Streams the order manifest and sequentially transmits each document.
 */
async function streamOrder(
  dc: RTCDataChannel,
  payload: SendOrderPayload,
  printTicket: string,
  onProgress: ProgressCallback
): Promise<void> {
  const {
    documents,
    token,
    sessionId,
    paymentMethod,
    paymentStatus,
    totalAmount,
    finishingServices,
  } = payload;
  const totalBytes = documents.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.file.size, 0);

  // 1. Send Order Manifest
  const manifest: TransferManifestMessage = {
    type: "order-manifest",
    sessionId,
    printTicket,
    order: {
      tokenCode: token.tokenCode,
      pickupPin: token.pickupPin,
      queuePosition: token.queuePosition,
      totalAmount,
      paymentMethod,
      paymentStatus,
      finishingServices: finishingServices.map((f) => ({
        type: f.type,
        label: f.label,
        price: f.price,
      })),
      totalFiles: documents.length,
    },
    files: documents.map((d) => ({
      fileId: d.id,
      fileName: d.file.name,
      fileSize: d.file.size,
      pageCount: d.pageCount,
      settings: d.settings,
    })),
  };

  dc.send(JSON.stringify(manifest));

  // Set backpressure threshold
  dc.bufferedAmountLowThreshold = 128 * 1024;
  let overallBytesSent = 0;

  // 2. Stream each document
  for (let i = 0; i < documents.length; i++) {
    const doc = documents[i];
    const file = doc.file;
    const totalChunks = Math.ceil(file.size / CHUNK_SIZE);

    // Send file header
    const fileHeader: TransferFileHeaderMessage = {
      type: "file-header",
      sessionId,
      fileId: doc.id,
      fileName: file.name,
      fileSize: file.size,
      totalChunks,
      pageCount: doc.pageCount,
      settings: doc.settings,
    };
    dc.send(JSON.stringify(fileHeader));

    onProgress({
      status: "sending",
      percent: totalBytes > 0 ? Math.round((overallBytesSent / totalBytes) * 100) : 0,
      bytesSent: overallBytesSent,
      totalBytes,
      currentFileIndex: i + 1,
      currentFileName: file.name,
      totalFiles: documents.length,
    });

    let offset = 0;
    while (offset < file.size) {
      // Check buffer drain if approaching threshold
      if (dc.bufferedAmount > dc.bufferedAmountLowThreshold) {
        await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
          if (dc.readyState !== "open") {
            return reject(new Error("DataChannel closed while awaiting buffer drain"));
          }
          const drainTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            if (dc.onbufferedamountlow) {
              dc.onbufferedamountlow = null;
            }
            if (dc.readyState === "open") resolve();
            else reject(new Error("DataChannel buffer drain timed out"));
          }, 5000);

          dc.onbufferedamountlow = () => {
            clearTimeout(drainTimer);
            dc.onbufferedamountlow = null;
            resolve();
          };
        });
      }

      const end = Math.min(offset + CHUNK_SIZE, file.size);
      const slice = file.slice(offset, end);
      const buf = await slice.arrayBuffer();

      dc.send(buf);
      overallBytesSent += (end - offset);
      offset = end;

      onProgress({
        status: "sending",
        percent: totalBytes > 0 ? Math.round((overallBytesSent / totalBytes) * 100) : 0,
        bytesSent: overallBytesSent,
        totalBytes,
        currentFileIndex: i + 1,
        currentFileName: file.name,
        totalFiles: documents.length,
      });
    }

    // Send file-eof for this file
    const fileEof: TransferFileEofMessage = {
      type: "file-eof",
      sessionId,
      fileId: doc.id,
    };
    dc.send(JSON.stringify(fileEof));
    // Also send legacy "EOF" string for older shop terminals
    dc.send("EOF");
  }

  // 3. Send order-complete message
  const orderComplete: TransferOrderCompleteMessage = {
    type: "order-complete",
    sessionId,
  };
  dc.send(JSON.stringify(orderComplete));
}

/**
 * Request an authoritative, collision-free local token directly from the Shop Desktop App.
 * Uses the Supabase signaling broadcast channel with zero DB count queries.
 */
export async function requestTokenFromShop(
  shopId: string,
  params: {
    sessionId: string;
    hasColor: boolean;
    totalAmount?: number;
    fileCount?: number;
  },
  timeoutMs: number = 3000
): Promise<QueueToken> {
  const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId);

  // 1. Ensure channel is in SUBSCRIBED state before transmitting
  await waitForChannelReady(channel, 1200);

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let settled = false;
    let retryInterval: any = null;
    let timeoutTimer: any = null;

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (retryInterval) clearInterval(retryInterval);
      if (timeoutTimer) clearTimeout(timeoutTimer);
    };

    const onTokenAssigned = ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const p = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      if (p.sessionId === params.sessionId && p.tokenCode) {
        if (!settled) {
          settled = true;
          cleanup();
          console.log(`[customer] Received authoritative local token from shop: ${p.tokenCode}`);
          resolve({
            tokenCode: p.tokenCode,
            queuePosition: p.queuePosition || 1,
            estimatedWaitMinutes: (p.queuePosition || 1) * 2,
          });
        }
      }
    };

    channel.on("broadcast", { event: "assign-token" }, onTokenAssigned);
    channel.on("broadcast", { event: "order-status" }, onTokenAssigned);

    const transmit = () => {
      if (!settled) {
        broadcast(channel, "request-token", {
          sessionId: params.sessionId,
          hasColor: params.hasColor,
          totalAmount: params.totalAmount,
          fileCount: params.fileCount,
        });
      }
    };

    // 2. Immediate transmit
    transmit();

    // 3. Rapid retry every 250ms in case first packet was dropped during connection
    retryInterval = setInterval(transmit, 250);

    // 4. Fallback if shop desktop takes longer than timeoutMs to respond
    timeoutTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!settled) {
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        console.warn("[customer] request-token timeout, using provisional local token");
        resolve({
          tokenCode: `#${params.hasColor ? 2 : 1}1`,
          queuePosition: 1,
          estimatedWaitMinutes: 2,
        });
      }
    }, timeoutMs);
  });
}
